1	"use server";
2	
3	import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
4	import { headers } from "next/headers";
5	
6	import { auth } from "@/lib/auth";
7	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
8	import {
9	  extensions,
10	  extensionVersions,
11	  extensionTags,
12	  files,
13	} from "@/lib/db/schema";
14	import { submit, VersionStateError } from "@/lib/extensions/state";
15	import { ManifestFormSchema, type ManifestFormValues } from "@/lib/validators/manifest";
16	
17	import { extractScanReason } from "@/lib/publish/scan-report";
18	
19	import {
20	  classifyDraftError,
21	  devErrorDetail,
22	  pgConstraint,
23	  pgErrorCode,
24	} from "./publish-errors";
25	
26	const DEFAULT_ORG_ID = "default";
27	
28	// Coerce empty / missing optional strings to `null`. We can't use `?? null`
29	// (it keeps `""`, which then FK-violates against `departments.id`) and we
30	// avoid `|| null` because it would also drop a legitimate `"0"`.
31	function emptyToNull(v: string | undefined | null): string | null {
32	  return v == null || v === "" ? null : v;
33	}
34	
35	export type CreateDraftResult =
36	  | { ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }
37	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
38	
39	export async function createDraftExtension(
40	  raw: ManifestFormValues,
41	): Promise<CreateDraftResult> {
42	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
43	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
44	
45	  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);
46	  if (!parsed.success) {
47	    // Return a stable error code (so the UI can localize it) and put the
48	    // human-readable Zod messages in `detail` so the user can see *what*
49	    // failed even outside development.
50	    return {
51	      ok: false,
52	      error: "invalid_input",
53	      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; "),
54	    };
55	  }
56	  const data = parsed.data;
57	
58	  const extensionId = crypto.randomUUID();
59	  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();
60	
61	  try {
62	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
63	      await tx.insert(extensions).values({
64	        id: extensionId,
65	        slug: data.slug,
66	        name: data.name,
67	        nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),
68	        tagline: emptyToNull(data.tagline),
69	        description: emptyToNull(data.description),
70	        descriptionZh: emptyToNull(data.descriptionZh),
71	        category: data.category,
72	        scope: data.scope,
73	        funcCat: data.funcCat,
74	        subCat: data.subCat,
75	        l2: emptyToNull(data.l2),
76	        // Empty strings are FK violations against departments.id — coerce to null.
77	        deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),
78	        homepageUrl: emptyToNull(data.homepageUrl),
79	        repoUrl: emptyToNull(data.repoUrl),
80	        licenseSpdx: emptyToNull(data.licenseSpdx),
81	        publisherUserId: session.user.id,
82	        ownerOrgId: DEFAULT_ORG_ID,
83	        visibility: "draft",
84	      });
85	
86	      await tx.insert(extensionVersions).values({
87	        id: versionId,
88	        extensionId,
89	        version: data.version,
90	        status: "pending",
91	      });
92	
93	      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
94	        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(
95	          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),
96	        );
97	      }
98	    });
99	  } catch (err) {
100	    const code = classifyDraftError(err);
101	    console.error("[publish] createDraftExtension failed", {
102	      code,
103	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
104	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
105	      slug: data.slug,
106	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
107	    });
108	    return { ok: false, error: code, detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
109	  }
110	
111	  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId };
112	}
113	
114	export type AttachFileResult =
115	  | { ok: true }
116	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
117	
118	export async function attachFile(
119	  versionId: string,
120	  r2Key: string,
121	  size: number,
122	  checksumSha256: string,
123	): Promise<AttachFileResult> {
124	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
125	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
126	
127	  const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
128	
129	  try {
130	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
131	      await tx.insert(files).values({
132	        id: fileId,
133	        r2Key,
134	        size: BigInt(size),
135	        checksumSha256,
136	        mimeType: "application/zip",
137	        scanStatus: "pending",
138	      });
139	      await tx
140	        .update(extensionVersions)
141	        .set({ bundleFileId: fileId })
142	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
143	    });
144	  } catch (err) {
145	    console.error("[publish] attachFile failed", {
146	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
147	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
148	      versionId,
149	      r2Key,
150	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
151	    });
152	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
153	  }
154	
155	  return { ok: true };
156	}
157	
158	export type SubmitResult =
159	  | { ok: true }
160	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
161	
162	export async function submitForReview(versionId: string): Promise<SubmitResult> {
163	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
164	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
165	
166	  // Load bundleFileId needed by the scan job
167	  const [version] = await db
168	    .select({ bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId })
169	    .from(extensionVersions)
170	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
171	    .limit(1);
172	
173	  if (!version?.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: "no_bundle" };
174	
175	  try {
176	    await submit(versionId);
177	  } catch (err) {
178	    console.error("[publish] submitForReview failed", {
179	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
180	      versionId,
181	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
182	    });
183	    // The state machine refused the transition — the version isn't in a
184	    // submittable state (already reviewed/rejected, or the row vanished).
185	    // This is not a generic DB error and shouldn't be reported as one.
186	    if (err instanceof VersionStateError) {
187	      return {
188	        ok: false,
189	        error: "version_not_submittable",
190	        detail: devErrorDetail(err),
191	      };
192	    }
193	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
194	  }
195	
196	  // Queue the scan job. If this fails (e.g. INNGEST_EVENT_KEY missing,
197	  // Inngest unreachable), roll the version status back to `pending` so it
198	  // doesn't sit stuck in `scanning` with no scan ever happening.
199	  try {
200	    const { inngest } = await import("@/lib/jobs/client");
201	    await inngest.send({
202	      name: "extension/scan.requested",
203	      data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
204	    });
205	  } catch (err) {
206	    console.error("[publish] inngest.send failed", err);
207	    try {
208	      await db
209	        .update(extensionVersions)
210	        .set({ status: "pending" })
211	        .where(
212	          and(
213	            eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),
214	            eq(extensionVersions.status, "scanning"),
215	          ),
216	        );
217	    } catch (rollbackErr) {
218	      console.error(
219	        "[publish] failed to roll back scanning -> pending after inngest error",
220	        rollbackErr,
221	      );
222	    }
223	    return {
224	      ok: false,
225	      error: "scan_queue_unavailable",
226	      detail: devErrorDetail(err),
227	    };
228	  }
229	
230	  return { ok: true };
231	}
232	
233	export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {
234	  // Pull the latest version per extension alongside the extension row so the
235	  // dashboard can show "where am I in the wizard" (e.g. needs upload vs.
236	  // submitted, awaiting scan) — visibility alone hides that info. Also
237	  // joins the bundle file via `bundleFileId` (logical reference, no FK)
238	  // so the dashboard can surface the scan rejection reason inline for
239	  // rejected versions without an extra round-trip.
240	  const rows = await db
241	    .select({
242	      id: extensions.id,
243	      slug: extensions.slug,
244	      name: extensions.name,
245	      category: extensions.category,
246	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
247	      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,
248	      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,
249	      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,
250	      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,
251	      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,
252	      latestScanReport: files.scanReport,
253	    })
254	    .from(extensions)
255	    .leftJoin(
256	      extensionVersions,
257	      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),
258	    )
259	    .leftJoin(files, eq(files.id, extensionVersions.bundleFileId))
260	    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))
261	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));
262	
263	  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the
264	  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per
265	  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.
266	  const byExtension = new Map<
267	    string,
268	    (typeof rows)[number] & { latestScanReason: string | null }
269	  >();
270	  for (const row of rows) {
271	    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) {
272	      byExtension.set(row.id, {
273	        ...row,
274	        latestScanReason: extractScanReason(row.latestScanReport),
275	      });
276	    }
277	  }
278	  return Array.from(byExtension.values());
279	}
280	
281	export type UpdateDraftResult =
282	  | { ok: true }
283	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
284	
285	// Update an in-flight draft's manifest fields. Owner-checked. Refuses to
286	// touch anything once the version has left `pending` — by then the
287	// scanner has likely picked it up and re-running scans is the worker's
288	// job, not the wizard's. `slug` and `version` can technically be
289	// changed, but the wizard locks them in resume mode because they form
290	// the R2 bundle key — letting them shift orphans the uploaded bundle.
291	export async function updateDraftExtension(
292	  extensionId: string,
293	  raw: ManifestFormValues,
294	): Promise<UpdateDraftResult> {
295	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
296	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
297	
298	  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);
299	  if (!parsed.success) {
300	    return {
301	      ok: false,
302	      error: "invalid_input",
303	      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; "),
304	    };
305	  }
306	  const data = parsed.data;
307	
308	  // Resolve the latest version + verify ownership + state in one read.
309	  const [row] = await db
310	    .select({
311	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
312	      versionId: extensionVersions.id,
313	      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,
314	    })
315	    .from(extensions)
316	    .leftJoin(
317	      extensionVersions,
318	      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),
319	    )
320	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
321	    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
322	    .limit(1);
323	
324	  if (!row || !row.versionId) {
325	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" };
326	  }
327	  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {
328	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" };
329	  }
330	  if (row.versionStatus !== "pending") {
331	    return { ok: false, error: "version_not_editable" };
332	  }
333	
334	  try {
335	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
336	      // SECURITY: `slug` and `version` are deliberately NOT written from
337	      // `data`. The wizard renders those inputs as `readOnly` in resume
338	      // mode, but `readOnly` is a client-side hint only — a caller
339	      // hitting this action directly (devtools, scripted POST) could
340	      // otherwise change the slug or version, which would orphan the
341	      // already-uploaded R2 bundle at its old `<slug>/<version>` key.
342	      // Rejecting mismatches outright would also work; silently keeping
343	      // the stored values is the smaller-surface choice and keeps the
344	      // resume flow forgiving when the form round-trips its locked
345	      // values back unchanged.
346	      await tx
347	        .update(extensions)
348	        .set({
349	          name: data.name,
350	          nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),
351	          tagline: emptyToNull(data.tagline),
352	          description: emptyToNull(data.description),
353	          descriptionZh: emptyToNull(data.descriptionZh),
354	          category: data.category,
355	          scope: data.scope,
356	          funcCat: data.funcCat,
357	          subCat: data.subCat,
358	          l2: emptyToNull(data.l2),
359	          deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),
360	          homepageUrl: emptyToNull(data.homepageUrl),
361	          repoUrl: emptyToNull(data.repoUrl),
362	          licenseSpdx: emptyToNull(data.licenseSpdx),
363	          updatedAt: new Date(),
364	        })
365	        .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));
366	
367	      // Tags: replace wholesale. The set is capped at 8 by the form
368	      // schema, so a delete-then-insert is cheaper and clearer than
369	      // computing a diff.
370	      await tx
371	        .delete(extensionTags)
372	        .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensionId));
373	      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
374	        await tx
375	          .insert(extensionTags)
376	          .values(data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })));
377	      }
378	    });
379	  } catch (err) {
380	    const code = classifyDraftError(err);
381	    console.error("[publish] updateDraftExtension failed", {
382	      code,
383	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
384	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
385	      extensionId,
386	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
387	    });
388	    return { ok: false, error: code, detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
389	  }
390	
391	  return { ok: true };
392	}

394	export type DiscardDraftResult =
395	  | { ok: true }
396	  | { ok: false; error: "not_found" | "unauthenticated" | "not_discardable" };
397	
398	// Permanently delete a draft extension. Owner-checked. Only allowed while
399	// visibility === "draft" — published or archived extensions are not
400	// throwaway. Cascading FK constraints clean up extension_versions,
401	// extension_tags, and (via versions) any associated files row.
402	//
403	// R2 bundle objects are intentionally left in place; orphan cleanup is
404	// the bucket lifecycle's job, not the user's.
405	export async function discardDraft(
406	  extensionId: string,
407	): Promise<DiscardDraftResult> {
408	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
409	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
410	
411	  const [row] = await db
412	    .select({
413	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
414	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
415	    })
416	    .from(extensions)
417	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
418	    .limit(1);
419	
420	  // Treat not-found and not-owner identically so the contract can't be
421	  // used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else.
422	  if (!row || row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {
423	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" };
424	  }
425	  if (row.visibility !== "draft") {
426	    return { ok: false, error: "not_discardable" };
427	  }
428	
429	  await db.delete(extensions).where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));
430	  return { ok: true };
431	}
432	
433	export async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {
434	  const [row] = await db
435	    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })
436	    .from(extensionVersions)
437	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
438	    .limit(1);
439	  return row?.status ?? null;
440	}

442	export type DraftSnapshot = {
443	  extensionId: string;
444	  versionId: string;
445	  slug: string;
446	  version: string;
447	  name: string;
448	  category: string;
449	  visibility: string;
450	  versionStatus: string;
451	  bundleUploaded: boolean;
452	  // Full manifest values for the form pre-fill in edit mode. Loaded by

454	  // — only the resume page reads this. Tag IDs come from a sibling
455	  // query against `extension_tags`.
456	  formValues: ManifestFormValues;

458	
459	export type GetDraftResult =
460	  | { ok: true; draft: DraftSnapshot }
461	  // Deliberately collapse non-owner to `not_found` so the public contract
462	  // can't be used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else's
463	  // account.
464	  | { ok: false; error: "not_found" | "unauthenticated" };
465	
466	// Load an extension + its latest version for the publish wizard's resume
467	// flow. Owner-checked: callers cannot read another user's draft. Returns
468	// just enough state for the wizard to land on the right step and render
469	// a read-only manifest summary.
470	export async function getDraft(extensionId: string): Promise<GetDraftResult> {
471	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
472	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
473	
474	  const [row] = await db
475	    .select({
476	      extensionId: extensions.id,
477	      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,
478	      slug: extensions.slug,
479	      name: extensions.name,
480	      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,
481	      tagline: extensions.tagline,
482	      description: extensions.description,
483	      descriptionZh: extensions.descriptionZh,
484	      category: extensions.category,
485	      scope: extensions.scope,
486	      funcCat: extensions.funcCat,
487	      subCat: extensions.subCat,
488	      l2: extensions.l2,
489	      deptId: extensions.deptId,
490	      homepageUrl: extensions.homepageUrl,
491	      repoUrl: extensions.repoUrl,
492	      licenseSpdx: extensions.licenseSpdx,
493	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
494	      versionId: extensionVersions.id,
495	      version: extensionVersions.version,
496	      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,
497	      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,
498	    })
499	    .from(extensions)
500	    .leftJoin(
501	      extensionVersions,
502	      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),
503	    )
504	    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))
505	    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))
506	    .limit(1);
507	
508	  if (!row || !row.versionId || !row.version || !row.versionStatus) {
509	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" };
510	  }
511	  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {
512	    // Same shape as a missing row; see comment on `GetDraftResult`.
513	    return { ok: false, error: "not_found" };
514	  }
515	
516	  // Tag IDs in a separate, very small query — extensions cap tags at 8
517	  // per the form schema, so this is at most 8 rows.
518	  const tagRows = await db
519	    .select({ tagId: extensionTags.tagId })
520	    .from(extensionTags)

522	  const tagIds = tagRows.map((r) => r.tagId);
523	
524	  return {
525	    ok: true,
526	    draft: {
527	      extensionId: row.extensionId,
528	      versionId: row.versionId,
529	      slug: row.slug,
530	      version: row.version,
531	      name: row.name,
532	      category: row.category,
533	      visibility: row.visibility,
534	      versionStatus: row.versionStatus,
535	      bundleUploaded: row.bundleFileId !== null,
536	      formValues: {
537	        slug: row.slug,
538	        name: row.name,
539	        nameZh: row.nameZh ?? "",
540	        version: row.version,
541	        category: row.category as ManifestFormValues["category"],
542	        scope: row.scope as ManifestFormValues["scope"],
543	        funcCat: row.funcCat as ManifestFormValues["funcCat"],

Also\n  // joins the bundle file via `bundleFileId` (logical reference, no FK)\n  // so the dashboard can surface the scan rejection reason inline for\n  // rejected versions without an extra round-trip.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n      latestScanReport: files.scanReport,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .leftJoin(files, eq(files.id, extensionVersions.bundleFileId))\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));\n\n  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the\n  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per\n  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.\n  const byExtension = new Map<\n    string,\n    (typeof rows)[number] & { latestScanReason: string | null }\n  >();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) {\n      byExtension.set(row.id, {\n        ...row,\n        latestScanReason: extractScanReason(row.latestScanReport),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n  return Array.from(byExtension.values());\n}\n\nexport type UpdateDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };\n\n// Update an in-flight draft's manifest fields. Owner-checked. Refuses to\n// touch anything once the version has left `pending` — by then the\n// scanner has likely picked it up and re-running scans is the worker's\n// job, not the wizard's. `slug` and `version` can technically be\n// changed, but the wizard locks them in resume mode because they form\n// the R2 bundle key — letting them shift orphans the uploaded bundle.\nexport async function updateDraftExtension(\n  extensionId: string,\n  raw: ManifestFormValues,\n): Promise<UpdateDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);\n  if (!parsed.success) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: \"invalid_input\",\n      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join(\"; \"),\n    };\n  }\n  const data = parsed.data;\n  const cls = defaultClassification(data.category);\n\n  // Resolve the latest version + verify ownership + state in one read.\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.versionStatus !== \"pending\") {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"version_not_editable\" };\n  }\n\n  try {\n    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n      // SECURITY: `slug` and `version` are deliberately NOT written from\n      // `data`. The wizard renders those inputs as `readOnly` in resume\n      // mode, but `readOnly` is a client-side hint only — a caller\n      // hitting this action directly (devtools, scripted POST) could\n      // otherwise change the slug or version, which would orphan the\n      // already-uploaded R2 bundle at its old `<slug>/<version>` key.\n      await tx\n        .update(extensions)\n        .set({\n          name: data.name,\n          nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),\n          tagline: data.summary,\n          taglineZh: emptyToNull(data.taglineZh),\n          category: data.category,\n          scope: data.scope,\n          funcCat: data.funcCat ?? cls.funcCat,\n          subCat: data.subCat ?? cls.subCat,\n          deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),\n          iconColor: data.iconColor,\n          readmeMd: emptyToNull(data.readmeMd ?? \"\"),\n          permissions: data.permissions ?? {},\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));\n\n      // Persist source method on the version too (the wizard re-sends it).\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ sourceMethod: data.sourceMethod })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, row.versionId!));\n\n      // Tags: replace wholesale. The set is capped at 8 by the form\n      // schema, so a delete-then-insert is cheaper and clearer than\n      // computing a diff.\n      await tx\n        .delete(extensionTags)\n        .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensionId));\n      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {\n        await tx\n          .insert(extensionTags)\n          .values(data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })));\n      }\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    const code = classifyDraftError(err);\n    console.error(\"[publish] updateDraftExtension failed\", {\n      code,\n      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),\n      constraint: pgConstraint(err),\n      extensionId,\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n    return { ok: false, error: code, detail: devErrorDetail(err) };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport type DiscardDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; error: \"not_found\" | \"unauthenticated\" | \"not_discardable\" };\n\n// Permanently delete a draft extension. Owner-checked. Only allowed while\n// visibility === \"draft\" — published or archived extensions are not\n// throwaway. Cascading FK constraints clean up extension_versions,\n// extension_tags, and (via versions) any associated files row.\n//\n// R2 bundle objects are intentionally left in place; orphan cleanup is\n// the bucket lifecycle's job, not the user's.\nexport async function discardDraft(\n  extensionId: string,\n): Promise<DiscardDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  // Treat not-found and not-owner identically so the contract can't be\n  // used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else.\n  if (!row || row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.visibility !== \"draft\") {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_discardable\" };\n  }\n\n  await db.delete(extensions).where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  return row?.status ?? null;\n}\n\nexport type DraftSnapshot = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  versionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  version: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  scope: string;\n  visibility: string;\n  versionStatus: string;\n  bundleUploaded: boolean;\n  // Full manifest values for the form pre-fill in edit mode. Loaded by\n  // `getDraft` regardless of state since the dashboard never shows them\n  // — only the resume page reads this. Tag IDs come from a sibling\n  // query against `extension_tags`.\n  formValues: ManifestFormValues;\n};\n\nexport type GetDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true; draft: DraftSnapshot }\n  // Deliberately collapse non-owner to `not_found` so the public contract\n  // can't be used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else's\n  // account.\n  | { ok: false; error: \"not_found\" | \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n// Load an extension + its latest version for the publish wizard's resume\n// flow. Owner-checked: callers cannot read another user's draft. Returns\n// just enough state for the wizard to land on the right step and render\n// a read-only manifest summary.\nexport async function getDraft(extensionId: string): Promise<GetDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      nameZh: extensions.nameZh,\n      tagline: extensions.tagline,\n      taglineZh: extensions.taglineZh,\n      readmeMd: extensions.readmeMd,\n      iconColor: extensions.iconColor,\n      permissions: extensions.permissions,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      scope: extensions.scope,\n      funcCat: extensions.funcCat,\n      subCat: extensions.subCat,\n      deptId: extensions.deptId,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n      sourceMethod: extensionVersions.sourceMethod,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId || !row.version || !row.versionStatus) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    // Same shape as a missing row; see comment on `GetDraftResult`.\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n\n  // Tag IDs in a separate, very small query — extensions cap tags at 8\n  // per the form schema, so this is at most 8 rows.\n  const tagRows = await db\n    .select({ tagId: extensionTags.tagId })\n    .from(extensionTags)\n    .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, row.extensionId));\n  const tagIds = tagRows.map((r) => r.tagId);\n\n  const iconColor = (row.iconColor ?? \"indigo\") as ManifestFormValues[\"iconColor\"];\n  const sourceMethod = (row.sourceMethod ?? \"zip\") as ManifestFormValues[\"sourceMethod\"];\n  const permissions = (row.permissions ?? {}) as ManifestFormValues[\"permissions\"];\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    draft: {\n      extensionId: row.extensionId,\n      versionId: row.versionId,\n      slug: row.slug,\n      version: row.version,\n      name: row.name,\n      category: row.category,\n      scope: row.scope,\n      visibility: row.visibility,\n      versionStatus: row.versionStatus,\n      bundleUploaded: row.bundleFileId !== null,\n      formValues: {\n        slug: row.slug,\n        name: row.name,\n        nameZh: row.nameZh ?? \"\",\n        version: row.version,\n        category: row.category as ManifestFormValues[\"category\"],\n        scope: row.scope as ManifestFormValues[\"scope\"],\n        funcCat: row.funcCat ?? undefined,\n        subCat: row.subCat ?? undefined,\n        deptId: row.deptId ?? \"\",\n        tagIds,\n        summary: row.tagline ?? \"\",\n        taglineZh: row.taglineZh ?? \"\",\n        readmeMd: row.readmeMd ?? \"\",\n        iconColor,\n        permissions,\n        sourceMethod,\n      },\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/validators/manifest.ts",
  "content": "import { z } from \"zod\";\n\n// Slug shape, exported so client-side form validators don't redefine it.\nexport const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;\nexport const SEMVER_PATTERN = /^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$/;\n\n// \"summary\" is the redesigned wizard's one-line description; it lands in\n// the existing `tagline` column. Capped at 80 chars to match the design.\nexport const SUMMARY_MAX = 80;\n\n// Single source of truth for what makes an extension manifest valid.\n// Mirrors `docs/manifest-spec.md`. Bundle and form schemas below derive\n// from this core; nothing should redefine these constraints elsewhere.\nexport const ExtensionManifestCore = z.object({\n  slug: z\n    .string()\n    .min(3)\n    .max(64)\n    .regex(SLUG_PATTERN, \"Lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens only\"),\n  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),\n  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),\n  version: z.string().regex(SEMVER_PATTERN, \"Must be semver (e.g. 1.0.0)\"),\n  category: z.enum([\"skills\", \"mcp\", \"slash\", \"plugins\"]),\n  scope: z.enum([\"personal\", \"org\", \"enterprise\"]),\n  funcCat: z.enum([\"workTask\", \"business\", \"tools\"]),\n  subCat: z.string().min(1).max(60),\n  l2: z.string().max(60).optional(),\n  description: z.string().min(1).max(280),\n  descriptionZh: z.string().max(280).optional(),\n  tagline: z.string().max(120).optional(),\n});\n\n// Permissions toggled in the wizard — surfaced on the detail page so users\n// know what the extension intends to do before installing.\nexport const PermissionsSchema = z\n  .object({\n    network: z.boolean().optional(),\n    files: z.boolean().optional(),\n    runtime: z.boolean().optional(),\n    data: z.boolean().optional(),\n  })\n  .default({});\n\n// Form: the slimmer field set the redesigned publish wizard collects.\n// Notable departures from the prior form:\n//   - `summary` (the design's one-line description, max 80) replaces the\n//     long `description`/`descriptionZh` pair. Stored in the `tagline` column.\n//   - `funcCat` / `subCat` / `l2` are gone from the wizard. Server-side\n//     defaults (`defaultClassification`) backfill funcCat/subCat from the\n//     chosen `category` so filters keep working.\n//   - URL/license fields (homepageUrl, repoUrl, licenseSpdx) are dropped\n//     from the wizard; the columns stay nullable in the DB.\n//   - `iconColor`, `readmeMd`, `permissions`, `sourceMethod`, `taglineZh`\n//     are new in the form and persist directly.\nexport const ManifestFormSchema = z.object({\n  slug: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.slug,\n  name: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.name,\n  nameZh: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.nameZh,\n  version: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.version,\n  category: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.category,\n  scope: ExtensionManifestCore.shape.scope,\n  summary: z.string().min(1, \"Required\").max(SUMMARY_MAX),\n  taglineZh: z.string().max(SUMMARY_MAX).optional(),\n  readmeMd: z.string().optional(),\n  iconColor: z\n    .enum([\"indigo\", \"amber\", \"emerald\", \"rose\", \"slate\"])\n    .default(\"indigo\"),\n  tagIds: z.array(z.string()).max(8),\n  deptId: z.string().optional(),\n  permissions: PermissionsSchema,\n  sourceMethod: z.literal(\"zip\").default(\"zip\"),\n});\n\nexport type ManifestFormValues = z.infer<typeof ManifestFormSchema>;\n\n// Bundle: same fields as before, lifted into the [extension] /\n// [categorization] TOML sections defined in docs/manifest-spec.md. The\n// bundle manifest still carries description + funcCat + subCat — those\n// come from whatever produced the bundle (CLI, hand-authored TOML), not\n// from the publish wizard, so they remain required at the bundle layer.\nexport const BundleManifestSchema = z.object({\n  extension: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({\n    slug: true,\n    name: true,\n    nameZh: true,\n    version: true,\n    category: true,\n    scope: true,\n    description: true,\n    descriptionZh: true,\n    tagline: true,\n  }),\n  categorization: ExtensionManifestCore.pick({\n    funcCat: true,\n    subCat: true,\n    l2: true,\n  }),\n});\n\nexport type BundleManifest = z.infer<typeof BundleManifestSchema>;\n"
}

wizard form no longer surfaces it.\nconst VALID_CORE = {\n  slug: \"my-skill\",\n  name: \"My Skill\",\n  version: \"1.0.0\",\n  category: \"skills\",\n  scope: \"personal\",\n  funcCat: \"workTask\",\n  subCat: \"search\",\n  description: \"Does things.\",\n} as const;\n\n// ---------- Shared core constraints ----------\n\ndescribe(\"ExtensionManifestCore\", () => {\n  describe(\"slug\", () => {\n    it(\"requires min 3 chars\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, slug: \"ab\" }).success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects > 64 chars\", () => {\n      const long = \"a\".repeat(65);\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, slug: long }).success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects uppercase\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, slug: \"My-Skill\" })\n          .success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"accepts hyphens between words\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, slug: \"my-cool-skill\" })\n          .success,\n      ).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"description\", () => {\n    it(\"is required at the core (bundle still requires description)\", () => {\n      const { description: _description, ...rest } = VALID_CORE;\n      expect(ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse(rest).success).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects > 280 chars\", () => {\n      const long = \"x\".repeat(281);\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, description: long })\n          .success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"name\", () => {\n    it(\"requires min 2 chars\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, name: \"x\" }).success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"version\", () => {\n    it(\"accepts semver\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, version: \"2.3.1\" })\n          .success,\n      ).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects non-semver\", () => {\n      for (const bad of [\"v1.0\", \"1.0\", \"latest\"]) {\n        expect(\n          ExtensionManifestCore.safeParse({ ...VALID_CORE, version: bad })\n            .success,\n        ).toBe(false);\n      }\n    });\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------- Form-specific extras ----------\n\ndescribe(\"ManifestFormSchema\", () => {\n  it(\"accepts a minimal valid manifest\", () => {\n    expect(ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(VALID_FORM).success).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"accepts all optional UI fields\", () => {\n    const result = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse({\n      ...VALID_FORM,\n      nameZh: \"我的技能\",\n      taglineZh: \"中文一句话\",\n      readmeMd: \"# Hello\\n\\nA README.\",\n      iconColor: \"amber\",\n      deptId: \"eng.cloud\",\n      tagIds: [\"search\", \"api\"],\n      permissions: { network: true, files: false },\n      sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n    });\n    expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  describe(\"summary\", () => {\n    it(\"is required\", () => {\n      const { summary: _s, ...rest } = VALID_FORM;\n      expect(ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(rest).success).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects > 80 chars\", () => {\n      const long = \"x\".repeat(81);\n      expect(\n        ManifestFormSchema.safeParse({ ...VALID_FORM, summary: long }).success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"iconColor\", () => {\n    it(\"defaults to indigo when omitted\", () => {\n      const result = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(VALID_FORM);\n      expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n      if (result.success) expect(result.data.iconColor).toBe(\"indigo\");\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects unknown colors\", () => {\n      expect(\n        ManifestFormSchema.safeParse({ ...VALID_FORM, iconColor: \"neon\" })\n          .success,\n      ).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"tagIds\", () => {\n    it(\"accepts up to 8 tags\", () => {\n      const tagIds = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => `tag${i}`);\n      expect(ManifestFormSchema.safeParse({ ...VALID_FORM, tagIds }).success).toBe(\n        true,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it(\"rejects more than 8 tags\", () => {\n      const tagIds = Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => `tag${i}`);\n      expect(ManifestFormSchema.safeParse({ ...VALID_FORM, tagIds }).success).toBe(\n        false,\n      );\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"sourceMethod\", () => {\n    it(\"defaults to zip\", () => {\n      const result = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(VALID_FORM);\n      expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n      if (result.success) expect(result.data.sourceMethod).toBe(\"zip\");\n    });\n\n    it(\"only accepts zip in v1\", () => {\n      // Git/CLI source UIs exist in the wizard but are not wired end-to-end shape ----------\n\ndescribe(\"BundleManifestSchema\", () => {\n  it(\"accepts a minimal valid bundle\", () => {\n    expect(BundleManifestSchema.safeParse(VALID_BUNDLE).success).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"rejects when [extension] section is missing\", () => {\n    const { extension: _e, ...rest } = VALID_BUNDLE;\n    expect(BundleManifestSchema.safeParse(rest).success).toBe(false);\n expect(BundleManifestSchema.safeParse(rest).success).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"inherits the slug regex from the core\", () => {\n    const bad = {\n      ...VALID_BUNDLE,\n      extension: { ...VALID_BUNDLE.extension, slug: \"Bad Slug\" },\n    };\n    expect(BundleManifestSchema.safeParse(bad).success).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"inherits the slug max-64 from the core\", () => {\n it(\"requires description at the bundle layer\", () => {\n    const { description: _d, ...extRest } = VALID_BUNDLE.extension;\n    const bad = { ...VALID_BUNDLE, extension: extRest };\n 1	// Security-critical regression tests: `getDraft` and `discardDraft` must
2	// treat a non-owner request identically to a missing row, so the public
3	// contracts can't be used to probe for someone else's extensions.
4	//
5	// We don't aim to fully cover the actions here — the DB-touching paths
6	// are best exercised end-to-end. These tests pin the ownership collapse
7	// because it's exactly the kind of thing a refactor can silently undo.
8	
9	import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
10	
11	const selectMock = vi.fn();
12	const deleteMock = vi.fn();
13	const transactionMock = vi.fn();
14	const getSessionMock = vi.fn();
15	
16	vi.mock("@/lib/db/client", () => ({
17	  db: {
18	    select: (...a: unknown[]) => selectMock(...a),
19	    delete: (...a: unknown[]) => deleteMock(...a),
20	    transaction: (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => transactionMock(cb),
21	  },
22	}));
23	
24	vi.mock("@/lib/auth", () => ({
25	  auth: {
26	    api: {
27	      getSession: (...a: unknown[]) => getSessionMock(...a),
28	    },
29	  },
30	}));
31	
32	vi.mock("next/headers", () => ({
33	  headers: () => Promise.resolve(new Headers()),
34	}));
35	
36	import {
37	  discardDraft,
38	  getDraft,
39	  updateDraftExtension,
40	} from "@/lib/actions/publish";
41	import type { ManifestFormValues } from "@/lib/validators/manifest";
42	
43	// Build a select chain whose `.where()` is BOTH thenable (for the simple
44	// `select(...).from(t).where(...)` shape) AND continues into `.orderBy`/
45	// `.limit` (for the joined-row shape). This lets the same mock cover
46	// both query patterns getDraft uses.
47	function selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {
48	  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);
49	  const orderBy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });
50	  const whereResult: {
51	    limit: typeof limit;
52	    orderBy: typeof orderBy;
53	    then: Promise<unknown[]>["then"];
54	  } = {
55	    limit,
56	    orderBy,
57	    then: ((onFulfilled, onRejected) =>
58	      Promise.resolve(rows).then(
59	        onFulfilled,
60	        onRejected,
61	      )) as Promise<unknown[]>["then"],
62	  };
63	  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(whereResult);
64	  const leftJoin = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
65	  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where, leftJoin });
66	  return { from, leftJoin, where, orderBy, limit };
67	}
68	
69	function deleteChain() {
70	  const where = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
71	  return { where };
72	}
73	
74	beforeEach(() => {
75	  vi.clearAllMocks();
76	});
77	
78	describe("discardDraft", () => {
79	  it("returns unauthenticated when no session", async () => {
80	    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue(null);
81	    const result = await discardDraft("ext-1");
82	    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" });
83	    expect(selectMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
84	    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
85	  });
86	
87	  it("returns not_found for a missing extension", async () => {
88	    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: "user-A" } });
89	    selectMock.mockReturnValue(selectChain([]));
90	    const result = await discardDraft("ext-missing");
91	    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "not_found" });
92	    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
93	  });
94	
95	  // Regression: non-owner MUST collapse to `not_found`, not a distinct
96	  // error — otherwise the contract leaks existence.
97	  it("returns not_found (not 'not_owner') when a different user owns it", async () => {
98	    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: "user-A" } });
99	    selectMock.mockReturnValue(
100	      selectChain([{ publisherUserId: "user-B", visibility: "draft" }]),

Second call: tag IDs.\n    selectMock\n      .mockReturnValueOnce(selectChain([fullManifestRow()]))\n      .mockReturnValueOnce(selectChain([{ tagId: \"t-1\" }, { tagId: \"t-2\" }]));\n\n    const result = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    if (!result.ok) return;\n    expect(result.draft.extensionId).toBe(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result.draft.bundleUploaded).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.slug).toBe(\"my-skill\");\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.name).toBe(\"My Skill\");\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.description).toBe(\"hello\");\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.tagIds).toEqual([\"t-1\", \"t-2\"]);\n    // Nullable DB columns surface as empty strings in the form (so the\n    // controlled <input>s don't go uncontrolled).\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.nameZh).toBe(\"\");\n    expect(result.draft.formValues.deptId).toBe(\"\");\n  });",

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { useState, useRef } from "react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { CheckCircle, Upload } from "lucide-react";
6	
7	import { ManifestForm } from "./manifest-form";
8	import {
9	  attachFile,
10	  createDraftExtension,
11	  submitForReview,
12	  updateDraftExtension,
13	} from "@/lib/actions/publish";
14	import type { ManifestFormValues } from "@/lib/validators/manifest";
15	import { Link } from "@/lib/i18n/navigation";
16	
17	type Step = 1 | 2 | 3;
18	
19	interface DraftState {
20	  extensionId: string;
21	  versionId: string;
22	  slug: string;
23	  version: string;
24	}
25	
26	// Render a thrown client-side error's message under the friendly headline,
27	// but only in development. Mirrors the server-side `devErrorDetail` helper.
28	function clientErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | null {
29	  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") return null;
30	  if (err instanceof Error) return err.message;
31	  return String(err);
32	}
33	
34	// Error codes the server actions can return. Anything else falls through to
35	// the generic "fallback" message.
36	const KNOWN_ERROR_CODES = new Set([
37	  "unauthenticated",
38	  "invalid_input",
39	  "slug_taken",
40	  "version_taken",
41	  "unique_conflict",
42	  "invalid_dept",
43	  "invalid_tag",
44	  "org_missing",
45	  "invalid_reference",
46	  "missing_required",
47	  "no_bundle",
48	  "version_not_submittable",
49	  "version_not_editable",
50	  "scan_queue_unavailable",
51	  "db_error",
52	]);
53	
54	// Subset of `DraftSnapshot` from the resume server action — kept here as a
55	// structural type so this component doesn't import server-only modules.
56	export interface ResumeDraft {
57	  extensionId: string;
58	  versionId: string;
59	  slug: string;
60	  version: string;
61	  name: string;
62	  category: string;
63	  bundleUploaded: boolean;
64	  // Full form values pre-fill Step 1 in resume mode, so a user can fix
65	  // a typo without discarding and recreating the draft.
66	  formValues: ManifestFormValues;
67	}
68	
69	interface UploadWizardProps {
70	  // When set, the wizard runs in "resume" mode: Step 1 is skipped, the
71	  // manifest details are shown read-only at the top of every step, and the
72	  // initial step is derived from whether the bundle is already uploaded.
73	  resume?: ResumeDraft;
74	}
75	
76	export function UploadWizard({ resume }: UploadWizardProps = {}) {
77	  const tw = useTranslations("publish.wizard");
78	  const tu = useTranslations("publish.upload");
79	  const tf = useTranslations("publish.form");
80	  const te = useTranslations("publish.errors");
81	
82	  const initialStep: Step = resume ? (resume.bundleUploaded ? 3 : 2) : 1;
83	
84	  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(initialStep);
85	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<DraftState | null>(
86	    resume
87	      ? {
88	          extensionId: resume.extensionId,
89	          versionId: resume.versionId,
90	          slug: resume.slug,
91	          version: resume.version,
92	        }
93	      : null,
94	  );
95	  const [fileUploaded, setFileUploaded] = useState(resume?.bundleUploaded ?? false);
96	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
97	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
98	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
99	  const [errorDetail, setErrorDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);
100	  const [uploadProgress, setUploadProgress] = useState<"idle" | "uploading" | "done">(
101	    resume?.bundleUploaded ? "done" : "idle",
102	  );
103	  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
104	  const isResume = resume !== undefined;
105	
106	  function clearError() {
107	    setError(null);
108	    setErrorDetail(null);
109	  }
110	
111	  // Translate a server-action error code into a localized message. Unknown
112	  // codes fall back to a generic message rather than leaking raw codes (e.g.
113	  // "db_error") to the user.
114	  function describeError(code: string): string {
115	    return KNOWN_ERROR_CODES.has(code) ? te(code) : te("fallback");
116	  }
117	
118	  // Reset the file-upload state. Called after a fresh draft is created so
119	  // a re-edited Step 1 doesn't leave the wizard thinking the previous
120	  // bundle is still attached to the new versionId.
121	  function resetUploadState() {
122	    setFileUploaded(false);
123	    setUploadProgress("idle");
124	    if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
125	  }
126	
127	  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
128	    clearError();
129	    try {
130	      // Edit mode: update the existing extension + version in place.
131	      // Slug and version are locked in the form so the bundle's R2 key
132	      // remains valid; we don't reset upload state here.
133	      if (resume) {
134	        const result = await updateDraftExtension(resume.extensionId, values);
135	        if (!result.ok) {
136	          setError(describeError(result.error));
137	          setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
138	          return;
139	        }
140	        // Mirror the latest values into local draft state so Step 3's
141	        // summary panel reflects what the user just saved.
142	        setDraft({
143	          extensionId: resume.extensionId,
144	          versionId: resume.versionId,
145	          slug: values.slug,
146	          version: values.version,
147	        });
148	        setStep(2);
149	        return;
150	      }
151	
152	      const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
153	      if (!result.ok) {
154	        setError(describeError(result.error));
155	        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
156	        return;
157	      }
158	      setDraft({
159	        extensionId: result.extensionId,
160	        versionId: result.versionId,
161	        slug: values.slug,
162	        version: values.version,
163	      });
164	      resetUploadState();
165	      setStep(2);
166	    } catch (err) {
167	      console.error("[publish] handleManifestSubmit threw", err);
168	      setError(te("fallback"));
169	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
170	    }
171	  }
172	
173	  async function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
174	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
175	    if (!file || !draft) return;
176	
177	    if (!file.name.endsWith(".zip")) { setError(tu("errorType")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
178	    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tu("errorSize")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
179	    clearError();
180	    setUploadProgress("uploading");
181	
182	    try {
183	      // 1. Get presigned URL
184	      const signRes = await fetch("/api/upload/sign", {
185	        method: "POST",
186	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
187	        body: JSON.stringify({ slug: draft.slug, version: draft.version, contentType: "application/zip", size: file.size }),
188	      });
189	      if (!signRes.ok) {
190	        // Try to read a `detail` from the error body — the route attaches
191	        // the underlying message in development to help diagnose missing
192	        // R2 config etc. Tolerate non-JSON bodies (e.g. proxy errors).
193	        let detail: string | null = null;
194	        try {
195	          const body = (await signRes.json()) as { detail?: string };
196	          detail = body.detail ?? null;
197	        } catch {
198	          /* non-JSON response, no detail */
199	        }
200	        setError(te("uploadSign"));
201	        setErrorDetail(detail);
202	        setUploadProgress("idle");
203	        return;
204	      }
205	      const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = await signRes.json() as { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string };
206	
207	      // 2. Upload directly to R2
208	      const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" }, body: file });
209	      if (!putRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadFailed")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
210	
211	      // 3. Record file in DB (checksum placeholder — real checksum done by Inngest scan)
212	      const attachResult = await attachFile(draft.versionId, r2Key, file.size, "pending");
213	      if (!attachResult.ok) {
214	        setError(describeError(attachResult.error));
215	        setErrorDetail(attachResult.detail ?? null);
216	        setUploadProgress("idle");
217	        return;
218	      }
219	
220	      setUploadProgress("done");
221	      setFileUploaded(true);
222	    } catch (err) {
223	      console.error("[publish] handleFileChange threw", err);
224	      setError(te("fallback"));
225	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
226	      setUploadProgress("idle");
227	    }
228	  }
229	
230	  async function handleSubmit() {
231	    if (!draft) return;
232	    setSubmitting(true);
233	    clearError();
234	    try {
235	      const result = await submitForReview(draft.versionId);
236	      if (!result.ok) {
237	        setError(describeError(result.error));
238	        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
239	        return;
240	      }
241	      setSubmitted(true);
242	    } catch (err) {
243	      console.error("[publish] handleSubmit threw", err);
244	      setError(te("fallback"));
245	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
246	    } finally {
247	      setSubmitting(false);
248	    }
249	  }
250	
251	  const stepLabels = [tw("step1Title"), tw("step2Title"), tw("step3Title")];
252	
253	  if (submitted) {
254	    return (
255	      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-16 text-center">
256	        <CheckCircle className="h-12 w-12 text-green-500" />
257	        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">{tw("successTitle")}</h2>
258	        <p className="text-muted-foreground max-w-sm">{tw("successBody")}</p>
259	        <Link href="/publish" className="mt-4 text-sm underline text-primary">
260	          {tw("backToDashboard")}
261	        </Link>
262	      </div>
263	    );
264	  }
265	
266	  return (
267	    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
268	      {/* Resume banner: surfaces what we're picking back up so the wizard
269	          isn't a context-free three-step UI in the middle of a flow. */}
270	      {resume && (
271	        <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
272	          <div className="min-w-0">
273	            <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
274	              {tw("resumingLabel")}
275	            </div>
276	            <div className="mt-0.5 truncate font-medium">{resume.name}</div>
277	            <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
278	              {resume.slug} · v{resume.version}
279	            </div>
280	          </div>
281	          <Link
282	            href="/publish"
283	            className="shrink-0 text-sm text-muted-foreground underline hover:text-foreground"
284	          >
285	            {tw("backToDashboard")}
286	          </Link>
287	        </div>
288	      )}
289	
290	      {/* Step indicator */}
291	      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-8">
292	        {([1, 2, 3] as Step[]).map((s) => (
293	          <div key={s} className="flex items-center gap-2">
294	            <span className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-semibold border ${
295	              s === step
296	                ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary"
297	                : s < step
298	                  ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700 border-green-300"
299	                  : "border-border text-muted-foreground"
300	            }`}>{s < step ? "✓" : s}</span>
301	            <span className={`text-sm ${s === step ? "font-medium" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
302	              {stepLabels[s - 1]}
303	            </span>
304	            {s < 3 && <span className="text-muted-foreground mx-1">›</span>}
305	          </div>
306	        ))}
307	      </div>
308	
309	      {error && (
310	        <div className="mb-4 rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
311	          <div>{error}</div>
312	          {errorDetail && (
313	            <pre className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-all rounded bg-red-100/70 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-xs text-red-900/80">
314	              {errorDetail}
315	            </pre>
316	          )}
317	        </div>
318	      )}
319	
320	      {/* Step 1: Manifest. In resume mode the form is pre-filled with the
321	          existing values and `slug` + `version` are locked (they form
322	          the R2 bundle key — letting them change orphans the bundle). */}
323	      {step === 1 && (
324	        <ManifestForm
325	          onSubmit={handleManifestSubmit}
326	          defaultValues={resume?.formValues}
327	          lockedFields={isResume ? ["slug", "version"] : undefined}
328	          submitLabel={isResume ? tw("saveAndContinue") : undefined}
329	        />
330	      )}
331	
332	      {/* Step 2: File upload */}
333	      {step === 2 && (
334	        <div className="space-y-6">
335	          <div
336	            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-border p-12 text-center cursor-pointer hover:border-primary transition-colors"
337	            onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
338	          >
339	            {uploadProgress === "done" ? (
340	              <>
341	                <CheckCircle className="h-10 w-10 text-green-500 mb-3" />
342	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("uploaded")}</p>
343	              </>
344	            ) : uploadProgress === "uploading" ? (
345	              <>
346	                <Upload className="h-10 w-10 text-primary mb-3 animate-pulse" />
347	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("uploading")}</p>
348	              </>
349	            ) : (
350	              <>
351	                <Upload className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground mb-3" />
352	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("dropzone")}</p>
353	                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{tu("maxSize")}</p>
354	              </>
355	            )}
356	            <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept=".zip" className="hidden" onChange={handleFileChange} />
357	          </div>
358	
359	          <div className="flex justify-between">
360	            {/* Step 1 is editable in both new and resume modes now, so
361	                Back simply walks back. The "Back to dashboard" link
362	                lives on the resume banner at the top of the wizard. */}
363	            <button
364	              type="button"
365	              onClick={() => setStep(1)}
366	              className="rounded-md border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-muted transition-colors"
367	            >
368	              {tw("backButton")}
369	            </button>
370	            <button
371	              type="button"
372	              disabled={!fileUploaded}
373	              onClick={() => setStep(3)}
374	              className="rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 transition-opacity"
375	            >
376	              {tw("nextButton")}
377	            </button>
378	          </div>
379	        </div>
380	      )}
381	
382	      {/* Step 3: Review & submit */}
383	      {step === 3 && draft && (
384	        <div className="space-y-6">
385	          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6 space-y-2 text-sm">
386	            <div className="flex justify-between">
387	              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{tf("slug")}</span>
388	              <span className="font-mono font-medium">{draft.slug}</span>
389	            </div>
390	            <div className="flex justify-between">
391	              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{tf("version")}</span>
392	              <span className="font-mono font-medium">{draft.version}</span>
393	            </div>
394	            <div className="flex justify-between">
395	              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{tf("bundle")}</span>
396	              <span className="text-green-600 font-medium">✓ {tu("uploaded")}</span>
397	            </div>
398	          </div>
399	
400	          <div className="flex justify-between">
401	            <button
402	              type="button"
403	              onClick={() => setStep(2)}
404	              className="rounded-md border border-border px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-muted transition-colors"
405	            >
406	              {tw("backButton")}
407	            </button>
408	            <button
409	              type="button"
410	              onClick={handleSubmit}
411	              disabled={submitting}
412	              className="rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 transition-opacity"
413	            >
414	              {submitting ? tw("submitting") : tw("submitButton")}
415	            </button>
416	          </div>
417	        </div>
418	      )}
419	    </div>
420	  );
421	}
422	
```

1	import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
2	import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";
3	
4	import { getSession } from "@/lib/auth/session";
5	import { getDraft } from "@/lib/actions/publish";
6	import { UploadWizard } from "@/components/publish/upload-wizard";
7	
8	export async function generateMetadata() {
9	  const t = await getTranslations("publish.wizard");
10	  return { title: t("resumingLabel") };
11	}
12	
13	interface PageProps {
14	  params: Promise<{ locale: string; id: string }>;
15	}
16	
17	export default async function PublishEditPage({ params }: PageProps) {
18	  const session = await getSession();
19	  if (!session) redirect("/sign-in");
20	
21	  const { id } = await params;
22	  const result = await getDraft(id);
23	  if (!result.ok) {
24	    // Both `not_found` and `not_owner` collapse to a 404 — we don't want
25	    // to leak whether an extension exists for someone else's account.
26	    notFound();
27	  }
28	
29	  const { draft } = result;
30	
31	  // Resume only makes sense for in-flight versions. If the version has
32	  // already been scanned/rejected/published, the user has nothing to do
33	  // here — bounce them back to the dashboard.
34	  if (draft.versionStatus !== "pending") {
35	    redirect("/publish");
36	  }
37	
38	  const t = await getTranslations("publish.wizard");
39	
40	  return (
41	    <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10">
42	      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold mb-8">
43	        {t("resumingLabel")}
44	      </h1>
45	      <UploadWizard
46	        resume={{
47	          extensionId: draft.extensionId,
48	          versionId: draft.versionId,
49	          slug: draft.slug,
50	          version: draft.version,
51	          name: draft.name,
52	          category: draft.category,
53	          bundleUploaded: draft.bundleUploaded,
54	          formValues: draft.formValues,
55	        }}
56	      />
57	    </main>
58	  );
59	}
60

3	import { Building2, ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
4	import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
5	import { useState } from "react";
6	
7	import {
8	  Popover,
9	  PopoverContent,
10	  PopoverTrigger,
11	} from "@/components/ui/popover";
12	import {
13	  DEPARTMENTS,
14	  deptPath,
15	  MY_DEPT_ID,
16	} from "@/lib/data/departments";
17	import { useFilters } from "@/lib/hooks/use-filters";
18	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
19	import type { Department } from "@/types";
20	
21	const ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN = "__all";
22	
23	export function DeptPicker() {
24	  const t = useTranslations("filters.dept");
25	  const { filters, update } = useFilters();
26	  // Default = MY_DEPT_ID per the prototype's behavior; explicit "__all"
27	  // disables the filter.
28	  const active = filters.dept ?? MY_DEPT_ID;
29	  const isAll = active === ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN;
30	  const isMine = active === MY_DEPT_ID;
31	  const path = isAll ? null : deptPath(active, "en");
32	
33	  function pick(id: string) {
34	    if (id === MY_DEPT_ID) {
35	      update({ dept: undefined }); // undefined falls back to default = MY_DEPT_ID
36	    } else {
37	      update({ dept: id });
38	    }
39	  }
40	
41	  const trigger = (
42	    <button
43	      type="button"
44	      className="bg-card border-border hover:border-primary/40 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] outline-none transition data-[popup-open]:border-primary"
45	    >
46	      <Building2 aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground size-3.5" />
47	      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
48	        {isMine && (
49	          <span aria-hidden className="bg-primary size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full" />
50	        )}
51	        {isAll ? (
52	          <span className="font-semibold">{t("all")}</span>
53	        ) : (
54	          path && (
55	            <>
56	              {path.length > 1 && (
57	                <span className="text-muted-foreground truncate text-[11.5px]">
58	                  {path.slice(0, -1).join(" / ")} /
59	                </span>
60	              )}
61	              <span className="font-semibold">{path[path.length - 1]}</span>
62	            </>
63	          )
64	        )}
65	      </span>
66	      <ChevronDown aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground size-3.5" />
67	    </button>
68	  );
69	
70	  return (
71	    <Popover>
72	      <PopoverTrigger render={trigger} />
73	      <PopoverContent
74	        className="w-[360px] p-0"
75	        align="start"
76	        sideOffset={6}
77	      >
78	        <div className="border-border flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-b p-2.5">
79	          <Chip active={isMine} onClick={() => pick(MY_DEPT_ID)}>
80	            <span
81	              aria-hidden
82	              className={cn(
83	                "size-1.5 rounded-full",
84	                isMine ? "bg-primary-foreground" : "bg-primary",
85	              )}
86	            />
87	            {t("mine")}
88	          </Chip>
89	          <Chip active={active === "eng"} onClick={() => pick("eng")}>
90	            {t("org")}
91	          </Chip>
92	          <Chip active={isAll} onClick={() => pick(ALL_DEPTS_TOKEN)}>
93	            {t("all")}
94	          </Chip>
95	        </div>
96	        <div className="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto py-1">
97	          <DeptTree
98	            list={DEPARTMENTS}
99	            depth={0}
100	            active={active}
101	            onPick={pick}
102	          />
103	        </div>
104	      </PopoverContent>
105	    </Popover>
106	  );
107	}
108	
109	function Chip({
110	  active,
111	  onClick,
112	  children,
113	}: {
114	  active: boolean;
115	  onClick: () => void;
116	  children: React.ReactNode;
117	}) {
118	  return (
119	    <button
120	      type="button"
121	      onClick={onClick}
122	      aria-pressed={active}
123	      className={cn(
124	        "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11.5px] font-semibold transition",
125	        active
126	          ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
127	          : "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-accent",
128	      )}
129	    >
130	      {children}
131	    </button>
132	  );
133	}
134	
135	interface DeptTreeProps {
136	  list: Department[];
137	  depth: number;
138	  active: string;
139	  onPick: (id: string) => void;
140	}
141	
142	function DeptTree({ list, depth, active, onPick }: DeptTreeProps) {
143	  return (
144	    <div>
145	      {list.map((d) => (
146	        <DeptNode
147	          key={d.id}
148	          dept={d}
149	          depth={depth}
150	          active={active}
151	          onPick={onPick}
152	        />
153	      ))}
154	    </div>
155	  );
156	}
157	
158	function DeptNode({
159	  dept,
160	  depth,
161	  active,
162	  onPick,
163	}: {
164	  dept: Department;
165	  depth: number;
166	  active: string;
167	  onPick: (id: string) => void;
168	}) {
169	  const t = useTranslations("filters.dept");
170	  // Auto-expand the path leading to MY_DEPT_ID.
171	  const initial =
172	    MY_DEPT_ID === dept.id || MY_DEPT_ID.startsWith(`${dept.id}.`);
173	  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(initial);
174	  const isActive = active === dept.id;
175	  const indent = 8 + depth * 14;
176	
177	  return (
178	    <>
179	      <div
180	        className="flex items-center"
181	        style={{ paddingLeft: indent }}
182	      >
183	        {dept.children ? (
184	          <button
185	            type="button"
186	            aria-label={`${expanded ? t("collapse") : t("expand")} ${dept.name}`}
187	            aria-expanded={expanded}
188	            onClick={(e) => {
189	              e.stopPropagation();
190	              setExpanded((p) => !p);
191	            }}
192	            className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded transition-colors"
193	          >
194	            {expanded ? (
195	              <ChevronDown aria-hidden className="size-3" />
196	            ) : (
197	              <ChevronRight aria-hidden className="size-3" />
198	            )}
199	          </button>
200	        ) : (
201	          <span aria-hidden className="size-5 shrink-0" />
202	        )}
203	        <button
204	          type="button"
205	          onClick={() => onPick(dept.id)}
206	          aria-pressed={isActive}
207	          className={cn(
208	            "mx-1 flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-left text-[12.5px] transition",
209	            isActive
210	              ? "bg-primary/10 text-primary font-semibold"
211	              : "text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
212	          )}
213	        >
214	          <span className="flex-1">{dept.name}</span>
215	          {dept.id === MY_DEPT_ID && (
216	            <span className="bg-primary/10 text-primary rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-bold tracking-wider">
217	              MINE
218	            </span>
219	          )}
220	        </button>
221	      </div>
222	      {dept.children && expanded && (
223	        <DeptTree
224	          list={dept.children}
225	          depth={depth + 1}
226	          active={active}
227	          onPick={onPick}
228	        />
229	      )}
230	    </>
231	  );
232	}
233

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/shared.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Check } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport type { ReactNode } from \"react\";\n\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\n// Form-row wrapper. Lays out a label, optional/required hint, content,\n// and helper text in the same vertical rhythm across every step.\nexport function PubField({\n  label,\n  hint,\n  required,\n  optional,\n  children,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  hint?: string;\n  required?: boolean;\n  optional?: string;\n  children: ReactNode;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-baseline gap-2\">\n        <label className=\"text-[13px] font-semibold\">\n          {label}\n          {required && <span className=\"ml-0.5 text-destructive\">*</span>}\n        </label>\n        {optional && (\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] font-normal text-muted-foreground\">\n            {optional}\n          </span>\n        )}\n      </div>\n      {children}\n      {hint && (\n        <div className=\"text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n          {hint}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// Single-line text input used everywhere except specialised pickers.\n// `mono` flips the family for slug/version/git-URL fields.\nexport function PubInput({\n  value,\n  onChange,\n  placeholder,\n  mono,\n  readOnly,\n  type = \"text\",\n}: {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (v: string) => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n  mono?: boolean;\n  readOnly?: boolean;\n  type?: \"text\" | \"url\";\n}) {\n  return (\n    <input\n      type={type}\n      value={value}\n      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n      placeholder={placeholder}\n      readOnly={readOnly}\n      className={cn(\n        \"w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-[13px] outline-none transition-shadow\",\n        \"focus:border-primary focus:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_color-mix(in_oklch,var(--primary)_18%,transparent)]\",\n        readOnly && \"cursor-not-allowed bg-muted text-muted-foreground\",\n        mono && \"font-mono\",\n      )}\n    />\n  );\n}\n\n// Multiline variant of PubInput. Mirrors the design's mono README field.\nexport function PubTextarea({\n  value,\n  onChange,\n  placeholder,\n  rows = 4,\n  mono,\n}: {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (v: string) => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n  rows?: number;\n  mono?: boolean;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <textarea\n      value={value}\n      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n      placeholder={placeholder}\n      rows={rows}\n      className={cn(\n        \"w-full resize-y rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-[13px] outline-none transition-shadow\",\n        \"focus:border-primary focus:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_color-mix(in_oklch,var(--primary)_18%,transparent)]\",\n        mono && \"font-mono\",\n      )}\n    />\n  );\n}\n\n// Selectable card used for type, scope, and source-method choices.\nexport function PubChoice({\n  value,\n  current,\n  onClick,\n  title,\n  desc,\n  disabled,\n  badge,\n}: {\n  value: string;\n  current: string | null | undefined;\n  onClick: () => void;\n  title: string;\n  desc?: string;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n  badge?: string;\n}) {\n  const active = value === current;\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={onClick}\n      disabled={disabled}\n      className={cn(\n        \"flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border border-[1.5px] px-3.5 py-3 text-left transition-all\",\n        active\n          ? \"border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]\"\n          : \"border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40\",\n        disabled && \"cursor-not-allowed opacity-60\",\n      )}\n    >\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n        <span className=\"text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-foreground\">\n          {title}\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          {badge && (\n            <span className=\"rounded-full bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground\">\n              {badge}\n            </span>\n          )}\n          {active && (\n            <span className=\"inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-primary-foreground\">\n              <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-2.5\" />\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      {desc && (\n        <span className=\"text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n          {desc}\n        </span>\n      )}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "// Icon-avatar palette used by both the wizard form and the live preview.\n// OKLCH values are mirrored from the prototype design (AgentCenter.html\n// `ICON_COLORS` near line 2608) so the rendered preview matches the\n// design canvas exactly.\n\nexport const ICON_COLOR_KEYS = [\n  \"indigo\",\n  \"amber\",\n  \"emerald\",\n  \"rose\",\n  \"slate\",\n] as const;\n\nexport type IconColor = (typeof ICON_COLOR_KEYS)[number];\n\nexport const ICON_COLORS: Record<IconColor, { bg: string; fg: string }> = {\n  indigo: { bg: \"oklch(58% 0.18 268)\", fg: \"#fff\" },\n  amber: { bg: \"oklch(72% 0.16 60)\", fg: \"#1a1408\" },\n  emerald: { bg: \"oklch(62% 0.14 160)\", fg: \"#fff\" },\n  rose: { bg: \"oklch(64% 0.16 12)\", fg: \"#fff\" },\n  slate: { bg: \"oklch(38% 0.02 260)\", fg: \"#fff\" },\n};\n"
}
