{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/live-preview.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\n\nimport { ICON_COLORS, type IconColor } from \"./icon-colors\";\n\n// Sticky right-rail preview that mirrors what the published listing card\n// will look like and the JSON manifest the CLI would consume. Pure\n// derivation from the current draft state — no fetching, no effects.\nexport function LivePreview({ draft }: { draft: ManifestFormValues }) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"publish.preview\");\n  const tType = useTranslations(\"publish.type\");\n  const ic = ICON_COLORS[(draft.iconColor ?? \"indigo\") as IconColor];\n  const initial = (draft.name?.[0] ?? \"A\").toUpperCase();\n  const summary = draft.summary || t(\"placeholderSummary\");\n\n  // Manifest mirror used in the side preview. Stable JSON serialisation —\n  // we strip empty strings and undefined permissions so the rendered\n  // panel doesn't churn on every keystroke when the user clears a field.\n  const manifest = JSON.stringify(\n    {\n      name: draft.slug || \"your-extension\",\n      displayName: draft.name || \"Your Extension\",\n      type: draft.category,\n      version: draft.version || \"0.0.0\",\n      scope: draft.scope,\n      description: draft.summary || \"\",\n      tags: draft.tagIds ?? [],\n      department: draft.deptId || undefined,\n      permissions: Object.entries(draft.permissions ?? {})\n        .filter(([, on]) => on)\n        .map(([k]) => k),\n    },\n    null,\n    2,\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n      <section>\n        <div className=\"mb-2 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground\">\n          {t(\"cardLabel\")}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-3.5\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-start gap-2.5\">\n            <div\n              aria-hidden\n              className=\"flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md font-display text-base font-bold\"\n              style={{ background: ic.bg, color: ic.fg }}\n            >\n              {initial}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n              <div className=\"truncate text-[13px] font-bold text-foreground\">\n                {draft.name || t(\"placeholderName\")}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n                {tType(draft.category)}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"min-h-[34px] text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n            {summary}\n          </div>\n          {draft.tagIds && draft.tagIds.length > 0 && (\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1\">\n              {draft.tagIds.slice(0, 4).map((tg) => (\n                <span\n                  key={tg}\n                  className=\"rounded bg-primary/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold text-primary\"\n                >\n                  {tg}\n                </span>\n              ))}\n              {draft.tagIds.length > 4 && (\n                <span className=\"text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">\n                  +{draft.tagIds.length - 4}\n                </span>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <section>\n        <div className=\"mb-2 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground\">\n          {t(\"manifestLabel\")}\n        </div>\n        <pre className=\"m-0 max-h-72 overflow-auto rounded-md border border-border bg-muted px-3 py-3 font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-foreground\">\n          {manifest}\n        </pre>\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Bolt, Globe, Plug, SlashSquare } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nimport { PubChoice, PubField, PubInput } from \"../shared\";\n\ntype Patch = Partial<ManifestFormValues>;\n\n// Slugify the user's name into the slug field. Same rules the design\n// uses (line 2669): lowercase, runs of non-alphanumerics collapse to\n// single hyphens, trim leading/trailing hyphens, cap at 40 chars.\nexport function slugFromName(name: string): string {\n  return name\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .trim()\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n    .replace(/^-|-$/g, \"\")\n    .slice(0, 40);\n}\n\nexport function BasicsStep({\n  draft,\n  patch,\n  lockedFields,\n}: {\n  draft: ManifestFormValues;\n  patch: (p: Patch) => void;\n  lockedFields?: ReadonlyArray<keyof ManifestFormValues>;\n}) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"publish.basics\");\n  const tType = useTranslations(\"publish.type\");\n  const tScope = useTranslations(\"publish.scope\");\n  const [zhOpen, setZhOpen] = useState(\n    Boolean(draft.nameZh) || Boolean(draft.taglineZh),\n  );\n\n  const isLocked = (k: keyof ManifestFormValues) =>\n    lockedFields?.includes(k) ?? false;\n\n  // Re-derive slug from name unless the user has hand-edited the slug\n  // (we detect that by checking whether the current slug matches what\n  // we'd derive — same rule as the design).\n  function setName(next: string) {\n    const auto = slugFromName(draft.name);\n    const update: Patch = { name: next };\n    if (!isLocked(\"slug\") && (!draft.slug || draft.slug === auto)) {\n      update.slug = slugFromName(next);\n    }\n    patch(update);\n  }\n\n  const types = [\n    { k: \"skills\" as const, icon: Bolt },\n    { k: \"mcp\" as const, icon: Globe },\n    { k: \"slash\" as const, icon: SlashSquare },\n    { k: \"plugins\" as const, icon: Plug },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-5\">\n      <PubField label={t(\"name\")} hint={t(\"nameHint\")} required>\n        <PubInput\n          value={draft.name}\n          onChange={setName}\n          placeholder={t(\"namePlaceholder\")}\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"slug\")} hint={t(\"slugHint\")} required>\n        <PubInput\n          value={draft.slug}\n          onChange={(v) => patch({ slug: v })}\n          placeholder=\"web-search-pro\"\n          mono\n          readOnly={isLocked(\"slug\")}\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"version\")} required>\n        <PubInput\n          value={draft.version}\n          onChange={(v) => patch({ version: v })}\n          placeholder=\"1.0.0\"\n          mono\n          readOnly={isLocked(\"version\")}\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"summary\")} required>\n        <PubInput\n          value={draft.summary}\n          onChange={(v) => patch({ summary: v })}\n          placeholder={t(\"summaryPlaceholder\")}\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={tType(\"label\")} required>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-4\">\n          {types.map(({ k, icon: Icon }) => {\n            const active = draft.category === k;\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={k}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => patch({ category: k })}\n                className={cn(\n                  \"flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[1.5px] px-2 py-3.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-all\",\n                  active\n                    ? \"border-primary bg-primary/[0.05] text-primary\"\n                    : \"border-border bg-card text-foreground hover:border-primary/40\",\n                )}\n              >\n                <Icon\n                  aria-hidden\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"size-[18px]\",\n                    active ? \"text-primary\" : \"text-muted-foreground\",\n                  )}\n                />\n                <span>{tType(k)}</span>\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={tScope(\"label\")} required>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2.5\">\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"personal\"\n            current={draft.scope}\n            onClick={() => patch({ scope: \"personal\" })}\n            title={tScope(\"personalTitle\")}\n            desc={tScope(\"personalDesc\")}\n          />\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"org\"\n            current={draft.scope}\n            onClick={() => patch({ scope: \"org\" })}\n            title={tScope(\"orgTitle\")}\n            desc={tScope(\"orgDesc\")}\n          />\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"enterprise\"\n            current={draft.scope}\n            onClick={() => patch({ scope: \"enterprise\" })}\n            title={tScope(\"enterpriseTitle\")}\n            desc={tScope(\"enterpriseDesc\")}\n          />\n        </div>\n      </PubField>\n\n      {/* Optional Chinese-translation panel. Collapsed by default; the\n          fields exist in the schema so anyone publishing for a bilingual\n          marketplace can still fill them in without leaving the wizard. */}\n      <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border bg-card/40 px-3.5 py-3\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={() => setZhOpen((v) => !v)}\n          className=\"flex w-full items-center justify-between text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground\"\n        >\n          <span>{t(\"zhToggle\")}</span>\n          <span className=\"text-[11px]\">{zhOpen ? \"−\" : \"+\"}</span>\n        </button>\n        {zhOpen && (\n          <div className=\"mt-3 flex flex-col gap-4\">\n            <PubField label={t(\"nameZh\")} optional={t(\"optional\")}>\n              <PubInput\n                value={draft.nameZh ?? \"\"}\n                onChange={(v) => patch({ nameZh: v })}\n                placeholder=\"例如：网页搜索 Pro\"\n              />\n            </PubField>\n            <PubField label={t(\"summaryZh\")} optional={t(\"optional\")}>\n              <PubInput\n                value={draft.taglineZh ?? \"\"}\n                onChange={(v) => patch({ taglineZh: v })}\n                placeholder=\"一句话简介\"\n              />\n            </PubField>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Check, Upload } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useRef, useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport { attachFile } from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nimport { PubChoice, PubField } from \"../shared\";\n\ntype SourceTab = \"zip\" | \"git\" | \"cli\";\n\nexport function SourceStep({\n  draft,\n  versionId,\n  bundleUploaded,\n  onBundleUploaded,\n  onError,\n}: {\n  draft: ManifestFormValues;\n  // Server-side identifiers needed for the upload — only available\n  // after Step 1 has persisted a draft.\n  versionId: string | null;\n  bundleUploaded: boolean;\n  onBundleUploaded: () => void;\n  // Surface upload-pipeline failures via the wizard's shared error rail.\n  onError: (msg: string, detail?: string | null) => void;\n}) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"publish.source\");\n  const tu = useTranslations(\"publish.upload\");\n  const te = useTranslations(\"publish.errors\");\n\n  const [tab, setTab] = useState<SourceTab>(\"zip\");\n  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<\"idle\" | \"uploading\" | \"done\">(\n    bundleUploaded ? \"done\" : \"idle\",\n  );\n  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState<string | null>(\n    bundleUploaded ? draft.slug + \" bundle\" : null,\n  );\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n\n  async function handleFile(file: File) {\n    if (!versionId) {\n      onError(te(\"invalid_input\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!file.name.endsWith(\".zip\")) {\n      onError(tu(\"errorType\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {\n      onError(tu(\"errorSize\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    setProgress(\"uploading\");\n    setFileName(file.name);\n    try {\n      const signRes = await fetch(\"/api/upload/sign\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          slug: draft.slug,\n          version: draft.version,\n          contentType: \"application/zip\",\n          size: file.size,\n        }),\n      });\n      if (!signRes.ok) {\n        let detail: string | null = null;\n        try {\n          const body = (await signRes.json()) as { detail?: string };\n          detail = body.detail ?? null;\n        } catch {\n          /* non-JSON response */\n        }\n        onError(te(\"uploadSign\"), detail);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = (await signRes.json()) as {\n        uploadUrl: string;\n        r2Key: string;\n      };\n      const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, {\n        method: \"PUT\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/zip\" },\n        body: file,\n      });\n      if (!putRes.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"uploadFailed\"));\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const attachResult = await attachFile(\n        versionId,\n        r2Key,\n        file.size,\n        \"pending\",\n      );\n      if (!attachResult.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"fallback\"), attachResult.detail ?? null);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      setProgress(\"done\");\n      onBundleUploaded();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(\"[publish] zip upload threw\", err);\n      onError(te(\"fallback\"));\n      setProgress(\"idle\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  function copy(idx: number, text: string) {\n    void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);\n    setCopied(idx);\n    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1500);\n  }\n\n  const cliCommands = [\n    {\n      label: t(\"cliStep1\"),\n      code: \"npm install -g @agentcenter/cli\",\n    },\n    { label: t(\"cliStep2\"), code: \"agentcenter login\" },\n    {\n      label: t(\"cliStep3\"),\n      code: `agentcenter publish --slug ${draft.slug || \"your-extension\"}`,\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-5\">\n      <PubField label={t(\"methodLabel\")} required>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-3\">\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"zip\"\n            current={tab}\n            onClick={() => setTab(\"zip\")}\n            title={t(\"zipTitle\")}\n            desc={t(\"zipDesc\")}\n          />\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"git\"\n            current={tab}\n            onClick={() => setTab(\"git\")}\n            title={t(\"gitTitle\")}\n            desc={t(\"gitDesc\")}\n            badge={t(\"comingSoon\")}\n          />\n          <PubChoice\n            value=\"cli\"\n            current={tab}\n            onClick={() => setTab(\"cli\")}\n            title={t(\"cliTitle\")}\n            desc={t(\"cliDesc\")}\n          />\n        </div>\n      </PubField>\n\n      {tab === \"zip\" && (\n        <div\n          onClick={() => versionId && fileRef.current?.click()}\n          onDragOver={(e) => {\n            e.preventDefault();\n          }}\n          onDrop={(e) => {\n            e.preventDefault();\n            const f = e.dataTransfer.files?.[0];\n            if (f) void handleFile(f);\n          }}\n          className={cn(\n            \"flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed p-8 transition-all\",\n            progress === \"done\"\n              ? \"border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]\"\n              : \"border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40\",\n            !versionId && \"cursor-not-allowed opacity-60\",\n            versionId && progress !== \"done\" && \"cursor-pointer\",\n          )}\n        >\n          {progress === \"done\" ? (\n            <>\n              <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-8 text-primary\" />\n              <div className=\"text-[14px] font-semibold\">\n                {fileName ?? tu(\"uploaded\")}\n              </div>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={(e) => {\n                  e.stopPropagation();\n                  setProgress(\"idle\");\n                  setFileName(null);\n                  if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = \"\";\n                }}\n                className=\"text-[12px] text-muted-foreground underline transition-colors hover:text-foreground\"\n              >\n                {t(\"replace\")}\n              </button>\n            </>\n          ) : progress === \"uploading\" ? (\n            <>\n              <Upload\n                aria-hidden\n                className=\"size-7 animate-pulse text-primary\"\n              />\n              <div className=\"text-[14px] font-semibold\">{tu(\"uploading\")}</div>\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <>\n              <Upload aria-hidden className=\"size-7 text-muted-foreground\" />\n              <div className=\"text-[14px] font-semibold\">{t(\"dropHere\")}</div>\n              <div className=\"rounded-md bg-primary px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground\">\n                {t(\"orBrowse\")}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted-foreground\">\n                {t(\"zipHint\")}\n              </div>\n            </>\n          )}\n          <input\n            ref={fileRef}\n            type=\"file\"\n            accept=\".zip\"\n            className=\"hidden\"\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              const f = e.target.files?.[0];\n              if (f) void handleFile(f);\n            }}\n          />\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {tab === \"git\" && (\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border bg-card/40 p-5\">\n          <div className=\"text-[13px] font-semibold text-foreground\">\n            {t(\"gitTitle\")}\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            {t(\"gitDeferred\")}\n          </div>\n          <input\n            disabled\n            placeholder={t(\"gitPlaceholder\")}\n            className=\"w-full cursor-not-allowed rounded-md border border-border bg-muted px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[13px] text-muted-foreground\"\n          />\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {tab === \"cli\" && (\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3.5\">\n          {cliCommands.map((c, i) => (\n            <div key={c.label}>\n              <div className=\"mb-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground\">\n                {i + 1}. {c.label}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted px-3 py-2.5 font-mono text-[12.5px]\">\n                <span className=\"truncate\">$ {c.code}</span>\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => copy(i, c.code)}\n                  className=\"inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded border border-border px-2 py-0.5 font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground\"\n                >\n                  {copied === i ? (\n                    <>\n                      <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-3 text-primary\" />\n                      {t(\"copied\")}\n                    </>\n                  ) : (\n                    t(\"copy\")\n                  )}\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Building2, Check, ChevronDown, X } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport {\n  Popover,\n  PopoverContent,\n  PopoverTrigger,\n} from \"@/components/ui/popover\";\nimport { DEPARTMENTS, deptPath } from \"@/lib/data/departments\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/lib/i18n/config\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\nimport type { Department } from \"@/types\";\n\nimport { ICON_COLORS, ICON_COLOR_KEYS, type IconColor } from \"../icon-colors\";\nimport { PubField, PubTextarea } from \"../shared\";\n\ntype Patch = Partial<ManifestFormValues>;\n\nconst PERMISSION_KEYS = [\"network\", \"files\", \"runtime\", \"data\"] as const;\ntype PermissionKey = (typeof PERMISSION_KEYS)[number];\n\nconst SUGGESTED_TAGS = [\n  \"stable\",\n  \"beta\",\n  \"official\",\n  \"ai\",\n  \"search\",\n  \"data\",\n  \"productivity\",\n];\n\nexport function ListingStep({\n  draft,\n  patch,\n  locale,\n}: {\n  draft: ManifestFormValues;\n  patch: (p: Patch) => void;\n  locale: Locale;\n}) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"publish.listing\");\n  const tPerm = useTranslations(\"publish.permissions\");\n  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const tags = draft.tagIds ?? [];\n  const initial = (draft.name?.[0] ?? \"A\").toUpperCase();\n  const ic = ICON_COLORS[(draft.iconColor ?? \"indigo\") as IconColor];\n\n  function addTag(raw: string) {\n    const v = raw.trim().toLowerCase();\n    if (!v || tags.includes(v) || tags.length >= 8) return;\n    patch({ tagIds: [...tags, v] });\n  }\n  function removeTag(tag: string) {\n    patch({ tagIds: tags.filter((x) => x !== tag) });\n  }\n  function togglePermission(key: PermissionKey) {\n    const cur = (draft.permissions ?? {}) as Record<PermissionKey, boolean>;\n    patch({ permissions: { ...cur, [key]: !cur[key] } });\n  }\n\n  const suggested = SUGGESTED_TAGS.filter((s) => !tags.includes(s));\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-5\">\n      <PubField label={t(\"icon\")} hint={t(\"iconHint\")} optional={t(\"optional\")}>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3.5\">\n          <div\n            aria-hidden\n            className=\"flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl font-display text-3xl font-bold\"\n            style={{ background: ic.bg, color: ic.fg }}\n          >\n            {initial}\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n            {ICON_COLOR_KEYS.map((c) => (\n              <button\n                key={c}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => patch({ iconColor: c })}\n                aria-label={c}\n                aria-pressed={draft.iconColor === c}\n                style={{ background: ICON_COLORS[c].bg }}\n                className={cn(\n                  \"h-[22px] w-[22px] rounded-md transition-all\",\n                  draft.iconColor === c\n                    ? \"ring-2 ring-foreground ring-offset-2 ring-offset-card\"\n                    : \"ring-1 ring-border\",\n                )}\n              />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"tags\")} hint={t(\"tagsHint\")} required>\n        <div className=\"flex min-h-[42px] flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-card p-2\">\n          {tags.map((tag) => (\n            <span\n              key={tag}\n              className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-primary/10 py-1 pl-2.5 pr-1 font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-primary\"\n            >\n              {tag}\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                aria-label={`Remove ${tag}`}\n                onClick={() => removeTag(tag)}\n                className=\"inline-flex p-0.5 text-primary hover:opacity-70\"\n              >\n                <X aria-hidden className=\"size-3\" />\n              </button>\n            </span>\n          ))}\n          <input\n            value={tagInput}\n            onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)}\n            onKeyDown={(e) => {\n              if (e.key === \"Enter\") {\n                e.preventDefault();\n                addTag(tagInput);\n                setTagInput(\"\");\n              } else if (\n                e.key === \"Backspace\" &&\n                tagInput === \"\" &&\n                tags.length > 0\n              ) {\n                removeTag(tags[tags.length - 1]);\n              }\n            }}\n            placeholder={t(\"tagsPlaceholder\")}\n            className=\"min-w-[120px] flex-1 border-none bg-transparent px-1.5 py-1 text-[13px] outline-none\"\n          />\n        </div>\n        {suggested.length > 0 && (\n          <div className=\"mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1\">\n            {suggested.slice(0, 6).map((s) => (\n              <button\n                key={s}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => addTag(s)}\n                className=\"rounded border border-dashed border-border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-foreground\"\n              >\n                + {s}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"dept\")} hint={t(\"deptHint\")}>\n        <DeptDropdown\n          value={draft.deptId ?? \"\"}\n          onChange={(id) => patch({ deptId: id || undefined })}\n          locale={locale}\n          placeholder={t(\"deptPlaceholder\")}\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={t(\"readme\")} hint={t(\"readmeHint\")} required>\n        <PubTextarea\n          value={draft.readmeMd ?? \"\"}\n          onChange={(v) => patch({ readmeMd: v })}\n          placeholder={t(\"readmePlaceholder\")}\n          rows={8}\n          mono\n        />\n      </PubField>\n\n      <PubField label={tPerm(\"label\")} hint={tPerm(\"hint\")}>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {PERMISSION_KEYS.map((k) => {\n            const on = Boolean(\n              (draft.permissions as Record<string, boolean> | undefined)?.[k],\n            );\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={k}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => togglePermission(k)}\n                aria-pressed={on}\n                className={cn(\n                  \"flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-left transition-all\",\n                  on\n                    ? \"border-primary bg-primary/[0.04]\"\n                    : \"border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/30\",\n                )}\n              >\n                <span\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"inline-flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border-[1.5px]\",\n                    on\n                      ? \"border-primary bg-primary\"\n                      : \"border-border bg-transparent\",\n                  )}\n                >\n                  {on && (\n                    <Check\n                      aria-hidden\n                      className=\"size-2.5 text-primary-foreground\"\n                    />\n                  )}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"text-[12.5px] text-foreground\">\n                  {tPerm(k)}\n                </span>\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </PubField>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// Lightweight, controlled department picker for the publish wizard.\n// Different from `components/filters/dept-picker` — that one is wired to\n// the URL filter state for browsing; this one is a plain controlled\n// input. Flat list with indented children, single-select.\nfunction DeptDropdown({\n  value,\n  onChange,\n  locale,\n  placeholder,\n}: {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (id: string) => void;\n  locale: Locale;\n  placeholder: string;\n}) {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const flat = flattenDepartments(DEPARTMENTS, locale);\n  const labelPath = value ? deptPath(value, locale).join(\" / \") : \"\";\n\n  return (\n    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>\n      <PopoverTrigger\n        render={\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-[13px] outline-none transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 focus:border-primary\"\n          >\n            <span className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-2 truncate\">\n              <Building2\n                aria-hidden\n                className=\"size-3.5 text-muted-foreground\"\n              />\n              <span className={cn(!value && \"text-muted-foreground\")}>\n                {value ? labelPath : placeholder}\n              </span>\n            </span>\n            <ChevronDown\n              aria-hidden\n              className=\"size-3 text-muted-foreground\"\n            />\n          </button>\n        }\n      />\n      <PopoverContent\n        align=\"start\"\n        sideOffset={4}\n        className=\"max-h-[260px] w-[var(--anchor-width)] overflow-auto p-1\"\n      >\n        {flat.map((d) => (\n          <button\n            key={d.id}\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => {\n              onChange(d.id);\n              setOpen(false);\n            }}\n            style={{ paddingLeft: 10 + d.depth * 16 }}\n            className={cn(\n              \"flex w-full items-center rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-[12.5px] transition-colors\",\n              value === d.id\n                ? \"bg-primary/10 font-semibold text-primary\"\n                : \"text-foreground hover:bg-accent\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {d.name}\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </PopoverContent>\n    </Popover>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction flattenDepartments(\n  list: Department[],\n  locale: Locale,\n  depth = 0,\n): Array<{ id: string; name: string; depth: number }> {\n  const out: Array<{ id: string; name: string; depth: number }> = [];\n  for (const d of list) {\n    out.push({\n      id: d.id,\n      name: locale === \"zh\" ? d.nameZh : d.name,\n      depth,\n    });\n    if (d.children) {\n      out.push(...flattenDepartments(d.children, locale, depth + 1));\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n"
}

65	      {...props}
66	    />
67	  )
68	}
69	
70	function PopoverDescription({
71	  className,
72	  ...props
73	}: PopoverPrimitive.Description.Props) {
74	  return (
75	    <PopoverPrimitive.Description
76	      data-slot="popover-description"
77	      className={cn("text-muted-foreground", className)}
78	      {...props}
79	    />
80	  )
81	}
82	
83	export {
84	  Popover,
85	  PopoverContent,
86	  PopoverDescription,
87	  PopoverHeader,
88	  PopoverTitle,
89	  PopoverTrigger,
90	}
91

deptPath(draft.deptId, locale).join(\" / \")\n    : null;\n  const permsOn = Object.entries(draft.permissions ?? {})\n    .filter(([, v]) => v)\n    .map(([k]) => tPerm(k));\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-5\">\n      <div className=\"text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n        {t(\"intro\")}\n      </div>\n\n      <SummaryCard title={t(\"basics\")}>\n        <Row label={tBasics(\"name\")} value={draft.name} onEdit={() => onJump(0)} />\n        <Row label={tBasics(\"slug\")} value={draft.slug} mono onEdit={() => onJump(0)} />\n        <Row\n          label={tBasics(\"version\")}\n          value={draft.version}\n          mono\n          onEdit={() => onJump(0)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tBasics(\"summary\")}\n          value={draft.summary}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(0)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tType(\"label\")}\n          value={tType(draft.category)}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(0)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tScope(\"label\")}\n          value={tScope(`${draft.scope}Title`)}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(0)}\n        />\n      </SummaryCard>\n\n      <SummaryCard title={t(\"source\")}>\n        <Row\n          label={tSource(\"methodLabel\")}\n          value={tSource(`${draft.sourceMethod}Title`)}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(1)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={t(\"bundle\")}\n          value={bundleUploaded ? t(\"bundleUploaded\") : t(\"bundleMissing\")}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(1)}\n        />\n      </SummaryCard>\n\n      <SummaryCard title={t(\"listing\")}>\n        <Row\n          label={tListing(\"tags\")}\n          value={draft.tagIds.length ? draft.tagIds.join(\", \") : null}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(2)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tListing(\"dept\")}\n          value={deptLabel}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(2)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tListing(\"readme\")}\n          value={\n            draft.readmeMd\n              ? `${draft.readmeMd.slice(0, 60)}${\n                  draft.readmeMd.length > 60 ? \"…\" : \"\"\n                }`\n              : null\n          }\n          onEdit={() => onJump(2)}\n        />\n        <Row\n          label={tPerm(\"label\")}\n          value={permsOn.length ? permsOn.join(\", \") : t(\"permsNone\")}\n          onEdit={() => onJump(2)}\n        />\n      </SummaryCard>\n\n      <div\n        className={cn(\n          \"flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3.5\",\n          willAutoPublish\n            ? \"border-emerald-300/60 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 dark:border-emerald-700/40 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-200\"\n            : \"border-amber-300/60 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900 dark:border-amber-700/40 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-200\",\n        )}\n      >\n        {willAutoPublish ? (\n          <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-5 shrink-0\" />\n        ) : (\n          <Clock aria-hidden className=\"size-5 shrink-0\" />\n        )}\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-0.5\">\n          <div className=\"text-[12.5px] font-semibold\">\n            {t(\"publishTo\")}: {tScope(`${draft.scope}Title`)}\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"text-[12px]\">\n            {willAutoPublish ? t(\"statusAuto\") : t(\"statusReview\")}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n        {t(\"terms\")}{\" \"}\n        <a className=\"text-primary hover:underline\" href=\"#\">\n          {t(\"termsLink\")}\n        </a>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SummaryCard({\n  title,\n  children,\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-1.5\">\n      <div className={SECTION_HEADER}>{title}</div>\n      <div>{children}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Row({\n  label,\n  value,\n  mono,\n  onEdit,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  value?: string | null;\n  mono?: boolean;\n  onEdit: () => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[140px_1fr_auto] items-start gap-3 border-t border-border px-3 py-2.5\">\n      <div className=\"text-[12px] font-medium text-muted-foreground\">\n        {label}\n      </div>\n      <div className={cn(\"text-[13px] text-foreground\", mono && \"font-mono\")}>\n        {value ?? <span className=\"italic text-muted-foreground/60\">—</span>}\n      </div>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={onEdit}\n        className=\"text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-primary hover:underline\"\n      >\n        Edit\n      </button>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/index.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { Check, ChevronLeft } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useLocale, useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\n\nimport {\n  createDraftExtension,\n  submitForReview,\n  updateDraftExtension,\n} from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\nimport type { Locale } from \"@/lib/i18n/config\";\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\nimport { LivePreview } from \"./live-preview\";\nimport { BasicsStep } from \"./steps/basics\";\nimport { ListingStep } from \"./steps/listing\";\nimport { ReviewStep } from \"./steps/review\";\nimport { SourceStep } from \"./steps/source\";\n\nconst STEP_KEYS = [\"basics\", \"source\", \"listing\", \"review\"] as const;\ntype StepIndex = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; // 4 = success\n\nconst KNOWN_ERROR_CODES = new Set([\n  \"unauthenticated\",\n  \"invalid_input\",\n  \"slug_taken\",\n  \"version_taken\",\n  \"unique_conflict\",\n  \"invalid_dept\",\n  \"invalid_tag\",\n  \"org_missing\",\n  \"invalid_reference\",\n  \"missing_required\",\n  \"no_bundle\",\n  \"version_not_submittable\",\n  \"version_not_editable\",\n  \"scan_queue_unavailable\",\n  \"db_error\",\n]);\n\n// Subset of `DraftSnapshot` returned by the resume server action — kept\n// here as a structural type so this client component doesn't import\n// server-only modules.\nexport interface ResumeDraft {\n  extensionId: string;\n  versionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  version: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  scope: string;\n  bundleUploaded: boolean;\n  formValues: ManifestFormValues;\n}\n\nconst EMPTY_DRAFT: ManifestFormValues = {\n  slug: \"\",\n  name: \"\",\n  nameZh: \"\",\n  version: \"1.0.0\",\n  category: \"skills\",\n  scope: \"personal\",\n  summary: \"\",\n  taglineZh: \"\",\n  readmeMd: \"\",\n  iconColor: \"indigo\",\n  tagIds: [],\n  deptId: \"\",\n  permissions: {},\n  sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n};\n\ninterface UploadWizardProps {\n  resume?: ResumeDraft;\n}\n\nexport function UploadWizard({ resume }: UploadWizardProps = {}) {\n  const tw = useTranslations(\"publish.wizard\");\n  const te = useTranslations(\"publish.errors\");\n  const locale = useLocale() as Locale;\n  const router = useRouter();\n\n  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ManifestFormValues>(\n    resume?.formValues ?? EMPTY_DRAFT,\n  );\n  // Whichever step we land on initially mirrors what is already saved:\n  // brand-new drafts start at Basics; a resume with an uploaded bundle\n  // can jump straight to Review; everything else lands on Source.\n  const [step, setStep] = useState<StepIndex>(() => {\n    if (!resume) return 0;\n    return resume.bundleUploaded ? 3 : 1;\n  });\n  const [extensionId, setExtensionId] = useState<string | null>(\n    resume?.extensionId ?? null,\n  );\n  const [versionId, setVersionId] = useState<string | null>(\n    resume?.versionId ?? null,\n  );\n  const [bundleUploaded, setBundleUploaded] = useState(\n    resume?.bundleUploaded ?? false,\n  );\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [errorDetail, setErrorDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  function patch(p: Partial<ManifestFormValues>) {\n    setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...p }));\n  }\n\n  function describeError(code: string) {\n    return KNOWN_ERROR_CODES.has(code) ? te(code) : te(\"fallback\");\n  }\n  function reportError(msg: string, detail?: string | null) {\n    setError(msg);\n    setErrorDetail(detail ?? null);\n  }\n  function clearError() {\n    setError(null);\n    setErrorDetail(null);\n  }\n\n  // Per-step validity. Drives the Next button and the rail check marks.\n  // Source is \"valid\" once the bundle has been uploaded — picking the\n  // method alone isn't enough to advance.\n  const stepsValid = [\n    Boolean(draft.name && draft.slug && draft.summary && draft.category && draft.scope),\n    bundleUploaded,\n    Boolean(draft.tagIds.length > 0 && draft.readmeMd),\n    true,\n  ];\n\n  // Advancing from Basics persists the draft (or updates an existing one),\n  // since downstream steps need a versionId to attach the bundle to and\n  // a stable slug for the R2 key.\n  async function advanceFromBasics() {\n    clearError();\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (extensionId) {\n        const result = await updateDraftExtension(extensionId, draft);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n      } else {\n        const result = await createDraftExtension(draft);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n        setExtensionId(result.extensionId);\n        setVersionId(result.versionId);\n      }\n      setStep(1);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function saveDraft() {\n    clearError();\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (extensionId) {\n        const result = await updateDraftExtension(extensionId, draft);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n      } else if (stepsValid[0]) {\n        const result = await createDraftExtension(draft);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n      } else {\n        // Nothing to save yet — bounce silently.\n      }\n      router.push(`/${locale}/publish`);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function submitFinal() {\n    if (!versionId) return;\n    clearError();\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      // Persist the latest Listing-step edits before submitting so the\n      // server-side review runs against what the user just saw.\n      if (extensionId) {\n        const upd = await updateDraftExtension(extensionId, draft);\n        if (!upd.ok) {\n          reportError(describeError(upd.error), upd.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n      const result = await submitForReview(versionId);\n      if (!result.ok) {\n        reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? null);\n        return;\n      }\n      setStep(4);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Success view — friendly card with scope-aware copy.\n  if (step === 4) {\n    const isAuto = draft.scope === \"personal\";\n    return (\n      <div className=\"mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col items-center gap-4 py-20 text-center\">\n        <div className=\"flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10\">\n          <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-10 text-primary\" />\n        </div>\n        <h2 className=\"font-display text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight\">\n          {tw(\"successTitle\")}\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"max-w-sm text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n          {isAuto ? tw(\"successAutoBody\") : tw(\"successReviewBody\")}\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"mt-3 flex gap-2\">\n          <Link\n            href=\"/publish\"\n            className=\"rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90\"\n          >\n            {tw(\"backToDashboard\")}\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const titleKey = STEP_KEYS[step];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-[1200px]\">\n      {/* Header — matches the design's centred title + close link + Save */}\n      <div className=\"mb-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-5\">\n        <div>\n          <Link\n            href=\"/publish\"\n            className=\"mb-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground\"\n          >\n            <ChevronLeft aria-hidden className=\"size-3\" />\n            {tw(\"backToDashboard\")}\n          </Link>\n          <h1 className=\"font-display text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight\">\n            {tw(\"title\")}\n          </h1>\n          <p className=\"mt-1.5 max-w-xl text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\">\n            {tw(\"subtitle\")}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={saveDraft}\n          disabled={busy}\n          className=\"rounded-md border border-border bg-transparent px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {tw(\"saveDraft\")}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {resume && (\n        <div className=\"mb-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-4 py-3\">\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <div className=\"text-[10.5px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground\">\n              {tw(\"resumingLabel\")}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mt-0.5 truncate text-[14px] font-semibold\">\n              {resume.name}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-muted-foreground\">\n              {resume.slug} · v{resume.version}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {error && (\n        <div className=\"mb-4 rounded-md border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/[0.06] px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-destructive\">\n          <div>{error}</div>\n          {errorDetail && (\n            <pre className=\"mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-all rounded bg-destructive/10 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-destructive/80\">\n              {errorDetail}\n            </pre>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Rail layout: 200px step rail · form · 320px live preview */}\n      <div className=\"grid gap-7 lg:grid-cols-[200px_1fr_320px]\">\n        <div className=\"hidden flex-col gap-1 lg:flex\">\n          {STEP_KEYS.map((key, i) => {\n            const done = i < step;\n            const active = i === step;\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={key}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => i <= step && setStep(i as StepIndex)}\n                disabled={i > step}\n                className={cn(\n                  \"flex items-start gap-3 rounded-md px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors\",\n                  active && \"bg-primary/[0.07]\",\n                  i > step && \"cursor-not-allowed\",\n                )}\n              >\n                <span\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"mt-0.5 inline-flex h-[22px] w-[22px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold\",\n                    active || done\n                      ? \"bg-primary text-primary-foreground\"\n                      : \"border-[1.5px] border-border text-muted-foreground\",\n                  )}\n                >\n                  {done ? <Check aria-hidden className=\"size-3\" /> : i + 1}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"flex flex-col\">\n                  <span\n                    className={cn(\n                      \"text-[13px]\",\n                      active\n                        ? \"font-bold text-foreground\"\n                        : \"font-medium text-muted-foreground\",\n                    )}\n                  >\n                    {tw(`${key}Title`)}\n                  </span>\n                  <span className=\"text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground\">\n                    {tw(`${key}Desc`)}\n                  </span>\n                </span>\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-6 py-7 sm:px-8\">\n          <h2 className=\"font-display mb-1 text-xl font-semibold\">\n            {tw(`${titleKey}Title`)}\n          </h2>\n          <p className=\"mb-6 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            {tw(`${titleKey}Desc`)}\n          </p>\n\n          {step === 0 && (\n            <BasicsStep\n              draft={draft}\n              patch={patch}\n              lockedFields={\n                resume && resume.bundleUploaded\n                  ? [\"slug\", \"version\"]\n                  : undefined\n              }\n            />\n          )}\n          {step === 1 && (\n            <SourceStep\n              draft={draft}\n              versionId={versionId}\n              bundleUploaded={bundleUploaded}\n              onBundleUploaded={() => setBundleUploaded(true)}\n              onError={reportError}\n            />\n          )}\n          {step === 2 && (\n            <ListingStep draft={draft} patch={patch} locale={locale} />\n          )}\n          {step === 3 && (\n            <ReviewStep\n              draft={draft}\n              locale={locale}\n              bundleUploaded={bundleUploaded}\n              onJump={(s) => setStep(s as StepIndex)}\n            />\n          )}\n\n          <div className=\"mt-7 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-5\">\n            <div className=\"text-[12px] text-muted-foreground\">\n              {tw(\"stepIndicator\", { current: step + 1, total: STEP_KEYS.length })}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1) as StepIndex)}\n                disabled={step === 0 || busy}\n                className=\"rounded-md border border-border bg-transparent px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-muted disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n              >\n                {tw(\"backButton\")}\n              </button>\n              {step < 3 ? (\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => {\n                    if (!stepsValid[step] || busy) return;\n                    if (step === 0) void advanceFromBasics();\n                    else setStep((s) => (s + 1) as StepIndex);\n                  }}\n                  disabled={!stepsValid[step] || busy}\n                  className=\"rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n                >\n                  {tw(\"nextButton\")}\n                </button>\n              ) : (\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => void submitFinal()}\n                  disabled={busy || !versionId || !bundleUploaded}\n                  className=\"rounded-md bg-primary px-5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n                >\n                  {busy ? tw(\"submitting\") : tw(\"submitButton\")}\n                </button>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <aside className=\"sticky top-4 hidden self-start lg:block\">\n          <div className=\"mb-3 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground\">\n            {tw(\"livePreview\")}\n          </div>\n          <LivePreview draft={draft} />\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/app/[locale]/publish/[id]/edit/page.tsx",
  "content": "import { notFound, redirect } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { getTranslations } from \"next-intl/server\";\n\nimport { getSession } from \"@/lib/auth/session\";\nimport { getDraft } from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\nimport { UploadWizard } from \"@/components/publish/upload-wizard\";\n\nexport async function generateMetadata() {\n  const t = await getTranslations(\"publish.wizard\");\n  return { title: t(\"resumingLabel\") };\n}\n\ninterface PageProps {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string; id: string }>;\n}\n\nexport default async function PublishEditPage({ params }: PageProps) {\n  const session = await getSession();\n  if (!session) redirect(\"/sign-in\");\n\n  const { id } = await params;\n  const result = await getDraft(id);\n  if (!result.ok) {\n    // Both `not_found` and `not_owner` collapse to a 404 — we don't want\n    // to leak whether an extension exists for someone else's account.\n    notFound();\n  }\n\n  const { draft } = result;\n\n  // Resume only makes sense for in-flight versions. If the version has\n  // already been scanned/rejected/published, the user has nothing to do\n  // here — bounce them back to the dashboard.\n  if (draft.versionStatus !== \"pending\") {\n    redirect(\"/publish\");\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"px-6 py-8 lg:px-10\">\n      <UploadWizard\n        resume={{\n          extensionId: draft.extensionId,\n          versionId: draft.versionId,\n          slug: draft.slug,\n          version: draft.version,\n          name: draft.name,\n          category: draft.category,\n          scope: draft.scope,\n          bundleUploaded: draft.bundleUploaded,\n          formValues: draft.formValues,\n        }}\n      />\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n"
}

1	import { and, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
2	
3	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
4	import { extensions, extensionVersions, files } from "@/lib/db/schema";
5	
6	export type ScanResult =
7	  | { ok: true; checksum: string; scanReport: unknown }
8	  | { ok: false; reason: string; scanReport: unknown };
9	
10	// Thrown when a transition is requested against a row that is missing or
11	// not in the expected source state. The state machine is:
12	//
13	//   pending --submit-->   scanning --recordScanResult--> ready | rejected
14	//   ready   --publishVersion--> ready (publishedAt stamped)
15	//
16	// Callers should treat this as "transition refused"; idempotent retries
17	// (e.g. duplicate Inngest deliveries) should catch and no-op.
18	export class VersionStateError extends Error {
19	  readonly code: "version_not_found";
20	  constructor(code: "version_not_found") {
21	    super(code);
22	    this.code = code;
23	    this.name = "VersionStateError";
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	// Move a version to scanning. Allowed source states are `pending` (the
28	// normal first submit) and `scanning` itself (an idempotent retry, e.g.
29	// when a previous attempt's Inngest send failed and left the row stuck —
30	// without this the user would have no way to recover). Versions already
31	// in `ready` or `rejected` reject the transition. Caller is responsible
32	// for kicking off the scan job (Inngest event) — this module owns DB state.
33	export async function submit(versionId: string): Promise<void> {
34	  const updated = await db
35	    .update(extensionVersions)
36	    .set({ status: "scanning" })
37	    .where(
38	      and(
39	        eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),
40	        inArray(extensionVersions.status, ["pending", "scanning"]),
41	      ),
42	    )
43	    .returning({ id: extensionVersions.id });
44	  if (updated.length === 0) throw new VersionStateError("version_not_found");
45	}
46	
47	// Apply the outcome of a bundle scan: file scan flag + version status.
48	// Both writes commit together; the version must currently be `scanning`.
49	export async function recordScanResult(
50	  versionId: string,
51	  fileId: string,
52	  result: ScanResult,
53	): Promise<void> {
54	  const [version] = await db
55	    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })
56	    .from(extensionVersions)
57	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
58	    .limit(1);
59	  if (!version || version.status !== "scanning") {
60	    throw new VersionStateError("version_not_found");
61	  }
62	
63	  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
64	    if (result.ok) {
65	      await tx
66	        .update(files)
67	        .set({
68	          scanStatus: "clean",
69	          scanReport: result.scanReport,
70	          checksumSha256: result.checksum,
71	        })
72	        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));
73	      await tx
74	        .update(extensionVersions)
75	        .set({ status: "ready" })
76	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
77	    } else {
78	      await tx
79	        .update(files)
80	        .set({ scanStatus: "flagged", scanReport: result.scanReport })
81	        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));
82	      await tx
83	        .update(extensionVersions)
84	        .set({ status: "rejected" })
85	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
86	    }
87	  });
88	}
89	
90	// Flip the version + its parent extension to published. Returns the
91	// extension id so the caller can revalidate caches and dispatch
92	// downstream events (those are orchestration, not state). The version
93	// must currently be `ready`.
94	//
95	// Note: extensions.search_vector is a GENERATED column maintained by
96	// Postgres; this module never writes to it.
97	export async function publishVersion(
98	  versionId: string,
99	): Promise<{ extensionId: string }> {
100	  const [row] = await db
101	    .select({
102	      extensionId: extensionVersions.extensionId,
103	      status: extensionVersions.status,
104	    })
105	    .from(extensionVersions)
106	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
107	    .limit(1);
108	
109	  if (!row || row.status !== "ready") {
110	    throw new VersionStateError("version_not_found");
111	  }
112	
113	  const { extensionId } = row;
114	  const now = new Date();
115	
116	  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
117	    await tx
118	      .update(extensions)
119	      .set({ visibility: "published", publishedAt: now })
120	      .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));
121	    await tx
122	      .update(extensionVersions)
123	      .set({ publishedAt: now })
124	      .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
125	  });
126	
127	  return { extensionId };
128	}
129	
```

1	import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from "vitest";
2	
3	const updateMock = vi.fn();
4	const selectMock = vi.fn();
5	const transactionMock = vi.fn();
6	
7	vi.mock("@/lib/db/client", () => ({
8	  db: {
9	    update: (...a: unknown[]) => updateMock(...a),
10	    select: (...a: unknown[]) => selectMock(...a),
11	    transaction: (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => transactionMock(cb),
12	  },
13	}));
14	
15	import {
16	  submit,
17	  recordScanResult,
18	  publishVersion,
19	  VersionStateError,
20	} from "@/lib/extensions/state";
21	
22	// Build a chain: update(table).set(values).where(predicate).returning(cols)
23	// Resolves to `returnedRows`.
24	function updateChain(returnedRows: unknown[] = []) {
25	  const returning = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(returnedRows);
26	  const where = vi
27	    .fn()
28	    .mockReturnValue({ returning, then: undefined as unknown as never });
29	  // For chains that don't call .returning(), the awaited shape is the where().
30	  // We wire it so `await chain.where(...)` resolves to undefined — Drizzle's
31	  // shape — by also making `where` thenable-friendly. The simpler path: tests
32	  // for non-returning updates just don't await directly here; we resolve via
33	  // a custom helper below.
34	  const set = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
35	  return { set, where, returning };
36	}
37	
38	// Build a select chain ending in .limit(rows).
39	function selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {
40	  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);
41	  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });
42	  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
43	  return { from, where, limit };
44	}
45	
46	// Fake tx exposing the same .update(...).set(...).where(...) shape.
47	function makeTx() {
48	  const updates: Array<{
49	    set: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
50	    where: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
51	  }> = [];
52	  const update = vi.fn().mockImplementation(() => {
53	    const where = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
54	    const set = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
55	    updates.push({ set, where });
56	    return { set };
57	  });
58	  return { tx: { update }, updates };
59	}
60	
61	beforeEach(() => {
62	  vi.clearAllMocks();
63	});
64	
65	describe("submit", () => {
66	  it("flips version status when current state is pending", async () => {
67	    const chain = updateChain([{ id: "v1" }]);
68	    updateMock.mockReturnValue({ set: chain.set });
69	
70	    await submit("v1");
71	
72	    expect(chain.set).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ status: "scanning" });
73	    // `where` must be called with a predicate (the and(eq, eq) clause).
74	    // We can't introspect the SQL builder directly, but we assert the call
75	    // happened with a non-undefined argument.
76	    expect(chain.where).toHaveBeenCalled();
77	    expect(chain.where.mock.calls[0][0]).toBeDefined();
78	    expect(chain.returning).toHaveBeenCalled();
79	  });
80	
81	  it("throws VersionStateError when no rows match (wrong state or missing)", async () => {
82	    const chain = updateChain([]); // returning -> no rows
83	    updateMock.mockReturnValue({ set: chain.set });
84	
85	    const err = await submit("v1").catch((e: unknown) => e);
86	    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(VersionStateError);
87	    expect((err as VersionStateError).code).toBe("version_not_found");
88	  });
89	});
90

  "old_string": "// Apply the outcome of a bundle scan: file scan flag + version status.\n// Both writes commit together; the version must currently be `scanning`.\nexport async function recordScanResult(\n  versionId: string,\n  fileId: string,\n  result: ScanResult,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const [version] = await db\n    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  if (!version || version.status !== \"scanning\") {\n    throw new VersionStateError(\"version_not_found\");\n  }\n\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    if (result.ok) {\n      await tx\n        .update(files)\n        .set({\n          scanStatus: \"clean\",\n          scanReport: result.scanReport,\n          checksumSha256: result.checksum,\n        })\n        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ status: \"ready\" })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));\n    } else {\n      await tx\n        .update(files)\n        .set({ scanStatus: \"flagged\", scanReport: result.scanReport })\n        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ status: \"rejected\" })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));\n    }\n  });\n}",
  "new_string": "// Apply the outcome of a bundle scan: file scan flag + version status.\n// Both writes commit together; the version must currently be `scanning`.\n//\n// Auto-publish on success: extensions with `scope = \"personal\"` skip the\n// admin-review step entirely — they go straight to `published` once their\n// bundle scan is clean. Org/Enterprise drafts stay in `ready` for an\n// admin to flip them via `publishVersion`.\nexport async function recordScanResult(\n  versionId: string,\n  fileId: string,\n  result: ScanResult,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const [version] = await db\n    .select({\n      status: extensionVersions.status,\n      extensionId: extensionVersions.extensionId,\n      scope: extensions.scope,\n    })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .innerJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionVersions.extensionId))\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  if (!version || version.status !== \"scanning\") {\n    throw new VersionStateError(\"version_not_found\");\n  }\n\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    if (result.ok) {\n      const isPersonal = version.scope === \"personal\";\n      const now = isPersonal ? new Date() : null;\n\n      await tx\n        .update(files)\n        .set({\n          scanStatus: \"clean\",\n          scanReport: result.scanReport,\n          checksumSha256: result.checksum,\n        })\n        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set(\n          isPersonal\n            ? { status: \"ready\", publishedAt: now }\n            : { status: \"ready\" },\n        )\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));\n      if (isPersonal) {\n        await tx\n          .update(extensions)\n          .set({ visibility: \"published\", publishedAt: now })\n          .where(eq(extensions.id, version.extensionId));\n      }\n    } else {\n      await tx\n        .update(files)\n        .set({ scanStatus: \"flagged\", scanReport: result.scanReport })\n        .where(eq(files.id, fileId));\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ status: \"rejected\" })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));\n    }\n  });\n}"
}
```

4	const selectMock = vi.fn();
5	const transactionMock = vi.fn();

7	vi.mock("@/lib/db/client", () => ({
8	  db: {
9	    update: (...a: unknown[]) => updateMock(...a),
10	    select: (...a: unknown[]) => selectMock(...a),
11	    transaction: (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => transactionMock(cb),
12	  },

16	  submit,
17	  recordScanResult,
18	  publishVersion,
19	  VersionStateError,
20	} from "@/lib/extensions/state";

22	// Build a chain: update(table).set(values).where(predicate).returning(cols)
23	// Resolves to `returnedRows`.
24	function updateChain(returnedRows: unknown[] = []) {
25	  const returning = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(returnedRows);
26	  const where = vi
27	    .fn()
28	    .mockReturnValue({ returning, then: undefined as unknown as never });
29	  // For chains that don't call .returning(), the awaited shape is the where().
30	  // We wire it so `await chain.where(...)` resolves to undefined — Drizzle's
31	  // shape — by also making `where` thenable-friendly. The simpler path: tests
32	  // for non-returning updates just don't await directly here; we resolve via
33	  // a custom helper below.
34	  const set = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
35	  return { set, where, returning };
36	}
37	
38	// Build a select chain ending in .limit(rows). Supports both the
39	// straight `.from().where().limit()` shape and the joined
40	// `.from().innerJoin().where().limit()` shape — `recordScanResult`
41	// uses the latter to fetch the parent extension's scope.
42	function selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {
43	  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);
44	  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });
45	  const innerJoin = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });
46	  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where, innerJoin });
47	  return { from, innerJoin, where, limit };
48	}
49	
50	// Fake tx exposing the same .update(...).set(...).where(...) shape.

  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nimport { BasicsStep, slugFromName } from \"./basics\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\nconst empty: ManifestFormValues = {\n  slug: \"\",\n  name: \"\",\n  nameZh: \"\",\n  version: \"1.0.0\",\n  category: \"skills\",\n  scope: \"personal\",\n  summary: \"\",\n  taglineZh: \"\",\n  readmeMd: \"\",\n  iconColor: \"indigo\",\n  tagIds: [],\n  deptId: \"\",\n  permissions: {},\n  sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n};\n\ndescribe(\"slugFromName\", () => {\n  it(\"lowercases, hyphenates, and trims\", () => {\n    expect(slugFromName(\"My Cool Extension\")).toBe(\"my-cool-extension\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"collapses non-alphanumerics into single hyphens\", () => {\n    expect(slugFromName(\"Hello   world!!  \")).toBe(\"hello-world\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"caps at 40 chars\", () => {\n    expect(slugFromName(\"a\".repeat(60))).toHaveLength(40);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"BasicsStep slug auto-derivation\", () => {\n  function setup(initial: Partial<ManifestFormValues> = {}) {\n    let draft: ManifestFormValues = { ...empty, ...initial };\n    const patch = vi.fn((p: Partial<ManifestFormValues>) => {\n      draft = { ...draft, ...p };\n    });\n    const view = render(<BasicsStep draft={draft} patch={patch} />);\n    const rerender = (next: Partial<ManifestFormValues>) => {\n      draft = { ...draft, ...next };\n      view.rerender(<BasicsStep draft={draft} patch={patch} />);\n    };\n    return { patch, rerender, getDraft: () => draft };\n  }\n\n  it(\"derives slug from name when slug is empty\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup();\n\n    await user.type(screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"namePlaceholder\"), \"X\");\n    // First keystroke fires patch({ name: \"X\", slug: \"x\" }).\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n      expect.objectContaining({ name: \"X\", slug: \"x\" }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"does NOT overwrite a user-edited slug\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup({ name: \"old-name\", slug: \"custom-slug\" });\n    // The slug \"custom-slug\" doesn't match slugFromName(\"old-name\"),\n    // so editing the name should leave the slug alone.\n    const nameInput = screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"namePlaceholder\");\n    await user.type(nameInput, \"X\");\n    const lastCall = patch.mock.calls.at(-1)?.[0];\n    expect(lastCall).toBeDefined();\n    expect(lastCall).not.toHaveProperty(\"slug\");\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"BasicsStep ZH translation toggle\", () => {\n  it(\"hides nameZh input by default and reveals it on click\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<BasicsStep draft={empty} patch={vi.fn()} />);\n    expect(screen.queryByPlaceholderText(\"例如：网页搜索 Pro\")).toBeNull();\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"zhToggle\" }));\n    expect(\n      screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"例如：网页搜索 Pro\"),\n    ).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"starts open when nameZh or taglineZh already has a value\", () => {\n    render(\n      <BasicsStep draft={{ ...empty, nameZh: \"中文名\" }} patch={vi.fn()} />,\n    );\n    expect(\n      screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"例如：网页搜索 Pro\"),\n    ).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}

  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\n\nimport { LivePreview } from \"./live-preview\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\nconst baseDraft: ManifestFormValues = {\n  slug: \"my-skill\",\n  name: \"My Skill\",\n  nameZh: \"\",\n  version: \"1.0.0\",\n  category: \"skills\",\n  scope: \"personal\",\n  summary: \"Real-time search\",\n  taglineZh: \"\",\n  readmeMd: \"\",\n  iconColor: \"indigo\",\n  tagIds: [\"search\", \"ai\"],\n  deptId: \"\",\n  permissions: { network: true },\n  sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n};\n\ndescribe(\"LivePreview\", () => {\n  it(\"shows the draft name, summary, and tag chips\", () => {\n    render(<LivePreview draft={baseDraft} />);\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"My Skill\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Real-time search\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"search\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"ai\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders manifest JSON with derived shape\", () => {\n    render(<LivePreview draft={baseDraft} />);\n    // Manifest preview is a <pre> blob — find the node and parse it.\n    const pre = document.querySelector(\"pre\");\n    expect(pre).not.toBeNull();\n    const manifest = JSON.parse(pre!.textContent ?? \"{}\");\n    expect(manifest).toMatchObject({\n      name: \"my-skill\",\n      displayName: \"My Skill\",\n      type: \"skills\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      scope: \"personal\",\n      tags: [\"search\", \"ai\"],\n      permissions: [\"network\"],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders only enabled permissions in the manifest\", () => {\n    render(\n      <LivePreview\n        draft={{\n          ...baseDraft,\n          permissions: { network: true, files: false, runtime: true },\n        }}\n      />,\n    );\n    const pre = document.querySelector(\"pre\");\n    const manifest = JSON.parse(pre!.textContent ?? \"{}\");\n    expect(manifest.permissions).toEqual([\"network\", \"runtime\"]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"falls back to placeholder name + summary when draft is empty\", () => {\n    render(\n      <LivePreview\n        draft={{ ...baseDraft, name: \"\", summary: \"\", tagIds: [] }}\n      />,\n    );\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"placeholderName\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"placeholderSummary\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}

from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nimport { ListingStep } from \"./listing\";\nimport type { ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n}));\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\n\nconst empty: ManifestFormValues = {\n  slug: \"my-skill\",\n  name: \"My Skill\",\n  nameZh: \"\",\n  version: \"1.0.0\",\n  category: \"skills\",\n  scope: \"personal\",\n  summary: \"x\",\n  taglineZh: \"\",\n  readmeMd: \"\",\n  iconColor: \"indigo\",\n  tagIds: [],\n  deptId: \"\",\n  permissions: {},\n  sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n};\n\nfunction setup(initial: Partial<ManifestFormValues> = {}) {\n  let draft: ManifestFormValues = { ...empty, ...initial };\n  const patch = vi.fn((p: Partial<ManifestFormValues>) => {\n    draft = { ...draft, ...p };\n  });\n  const view = render(\n    <ListingStep draft={draft} patch={patch} locale=\"en\" />,\n  );\n  const rerender = (next: Partial<ManifestFormValues>) => {\n    draft = { ...draft, ...next };\n    view.rerender(<ListingStep draft={draft} patch={patch} locale=\"en\" />);\n  };\n  return { patch, rerender, getDraft: () => draft };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"ListingStep tags\", () => {\n  it(\"adds a tag on Enter and clears the input\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup();\n    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"tagsPlaceholder\");\n    await user.type(input, \"search{enter}\");\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tagIds: [\"search\"] });\n  });\n\n  it(\"removes the trailing tag on Backspace when input is empty\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup({ tagIds: [\"a\", \"b\"] });\n    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"tagsPlaceholder\");\n    input.focus();\n    await user.keyboard(\"{Backspace}\");\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tagIds: [\"a\"] });\n  });\n\n  it(\"adds a suggested tag when its chip is clicked\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup();\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"+ stable\" }));\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ tagIds: [\"stable\"] });\n  });\n\n  it(\"ignores duplicate tag adds\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup({ tagIds: [\"search\"] });\n    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText(\"tagsPlaceholder\");\n    await user.type(input, \"search{enter}\");\n    expect(patch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"ListingStep permissions\", () => {\n  it(\"toggles a permission on click\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup();\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"network\" }));\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ permissions: { network: true } });\n  });\n\n  it(\"flips a permission off when already on\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const { patch } = setup({\n      permissions: { network: true, files: true },\n    });\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"network\" }));\n    expect(patch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n      permissions: { network: false, files: true },\n    });\n  });\n});\n"
}

360	            → Zod validate → server action createDraftExtension() → returns extensionId
361	  Step 2: File upload     POST /api/upload/sign  → R2 presigned PUT
362	                          → browser uploads bundle directly to R2
363	                          → server action attachFile(extensionId, fileId)
364	  Step 3: Review & submit → server action submitForReview(extensionId)
365	                          → submit(versionId) flips status to 'scanning'
366	                            (idempotent: pending|scanning → scanning, so a retry
367	                             after a failed inngest.send can re-queue the scan)
368	                          → inngest.send({ name: 'extension/scan.requested', ... })
369	                            (best-effort rollback to 'pending' on send failure)
370	                          → return to /publish dashboard
371	
372	[Publisher] /publish (dashboard)
373	  - Lists my drafts with stage (needs upload / ready to submit / scanning / …).
374	  - Resumable rows (status='pending') link to /publish/[id]/edit → same wizard,
375	    pre-filled, slug+version locked while a bundle is uploaded (they form the
376	    R2 bundle key bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip).
377	  - Per-row Discard button hard-deletes the draft (FK cascades). R2 bundle
378	    objects are left for bucket lifecycle.
379	
380	[Background, Inngest]
381	  on 'extension/scan.requested':
382	    1. fetch from R2
383	    2. checksum + size guard
384	    3. parse manifest.json against ManifestSchema (Zod)
385	    4. (optional) clamav scan
386	    5. write scanReport to files; on clean → status='ready'
387	    6. inngest.send({ name: 'extension/index.requested', ... })
388	  on 'extension/index.requested':
389	    1. UPDATE extensions SET searchVector = ...
390	    2. flip visibility='published' if all checks pass
391	    3. revalidateTag('extensions')

393	```
394	
395	**Synchronous** (server action, returns < 200ms): manifest creation, presigned URL signing, attach-file, submitForReview enqueue.
396	**Async** (Inngest): bundle download, scan, validation, indexing, publish flip.
