All four phases done. Per project workflow I'll pause for your go-ahead before committing. Here's the summary of what landed and the proposed commit plan.

## What's in the branch `feat/publish-redesign`

**Phase P1 — schema + validators + actions** (8 files)
- `lib/db/schema/extension.ts`: added `permissions jsonb`, `extension_versions.sourceMethod text`, `sourceMeta jsonb`; made `funcCat`/`subCat` nullable
- `drizzle/0002_thick_madame_hydra.sql` + journal/snapshot — migration ready to apply (search_vector line removed; the FTS column is still hand-applied via `db:apply-fts` per project memory)
- `lib/validators/manifest.ts`: form schema slimmed; `summary` (≤80 chars, maps to `tagline` column), `permissions`, `iconColor`, `sourceMethod` added; URL/license/funcCat fields dropped from form (kept in core for bundle TOML)
- `lib/actions/publish.ts`: `defaultClassification(category)` helper backfills funcCat/subCat; create/update/getDraft now wired to the new shape
- Tests updated: `manifest.test.ts`, `publish-ownership.test.ts`

**Phase P2 — rail wizard UI** (11 files)
- New `components/publish/wizard/`: `index.tsx`, `shared.tsx`, `live-preview.tsx`, `icon-colors.ts`, `steps/{basics,source,listing,review}.tsx` — 200px rail · form · 320px sticky preview, 4 steps + scope-aware success page, save-draft button, slug auto-derivation
- Old `components/publish/manifest-form.tsx` + test deleted
- `components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx` is now a re-export so page imports keep working
- `app/[locale]/publish/{new,[id]/edit}/page.tsx`: simpler wrappers (wizard owns its own header)
- `lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json`: ~50 new keys under `publish.{wizard,basics,source,listing,review,permissions,type,scope,preview,upload}`

**Phase P3 — auto-publish** (2 files)
- `lib/extensions/state.ts`: `recordScanResult` joins extensions to read `scope`; on `ok` + `scope === "personal"` it stamps `extensionVersions.publishedAt` and flips `extensions.visibility = "published"` in the same transaction. Org/Enterprise unchanged.
- `lib/extensions/state.test.ts`: split into org-stays-ready and personal-auto-publishes cases

**Phase P4 — tests + docs** (4 files)
- New tests: `wizard/steps/basics.test.tsx` (slug derivation + ZH toggle), `wizard/steps/listing.test.tsx` (tag pills + permissions), `wizard/live-preview.test.tsx` (manifest derivation)
- `docs/plan.md`: publish-flow section rewritten for the 4-step wizard + auto-publish path

## Verification done

- `bun run validate` (lint + typecheck + 279 tests across 32 files) — clean
- `bun run build` — clean, all `/publish/*` routes registered
- Migration **not applied** to your DB yet — it's reversible-only-on-rollback so I left that to you. Apply with `bun run db:migrate && bun run db:apply-fts` (the latter per memory note for the FTS column on fresh DBs).
- Manual UI walk in a browser still pending — wants Inngest dev server running.

## Proposed commits

1. `feat(publish-p1): nullable funcCat/subCat + add permissions/source columns + slimmed form schema`
2. `feat(publish-p2): redesigned 4-step rail wizard with live preview pane`
3. `feat(publish-p3): auto-publish personal-scope versions on scan success`
4. `docs(publish): update plan.md for redesigned wizard + auto-publish path`

Each phase keeps tests + i18n + code together. Ready to commit when you give the word.

Steps: Basics → Source → Listing → Review. Drops the prototype's classic + onepage variants (rail-only).\n- **Field set** — name (with auto-derived slug), summary (≤80, maps to existing `tagline` column), type 4-card grid, scope 3-card stack, optional ZH translation panel. CLI tab shows 3 copy-able commands.\n- **Auto-publish** — `recordScanResult` joins extensions to read scope; on a clean scan, `scope = personal` flips visibility to `published` and stamps `publishedAt` in the same transaction. Org/Enterprise stay at `ready` for admin review (existing behavior).\n- **Schema** — `extensions.permissions`, `extension_versions.sourceMethod`, `extension_versions.sourceMeta` added; `funcCat`/`subCat` made nullable (server defaults backfill from `category` for new drafts).\n- **i18n** — ~50 new keys under `publish.*`, full EN + ZH parity.\n\n## Commits\n\n1. `feat(publish-p1)` — schema + validators + actions\n2. `feat(publish-p2)` — rail wizard UI + i18n + page wrappers + tests\n3. `feat(publish-p3)` — auto-publish personal-scope on scan success\n4. `docs(publish)` — plan.md narrative update\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `bun run validate` — lint + typecheck + 279/279 tests\n- [x] `bun run build` — clean\n- [ ] Apply migration: `bun run db:migrate && bun run db:apply-fts`\n- [ ] Local walk-through with Inngest dev (`npx inngest-cli@latest dev`):\n  - [ ] `/en/publish/new` — walk all 4 steps with a small zip; confirm rail/preview layout\n  - [ ] `/zh/publish/new` — Chinese strings render\n  - [ ] Resume an in-progress draft via the dashboard; slug + version locked\n  - [ ] Save Draft mid-flow → bounces to dashboard\n  - [ ] Personal-scope submit → version published immediately on scan-pass\n  - [ ] Org-scope submit → stays at `ready`\n  - [ ] Bad zip → `rejected` row with reason inline (regression on `extractScanReason`)\n\n## What this PR deliberately does NOT do\n\n- Git source method (UI shell only — `<button disabled>` with a \"Soon\" badge). }

null;\n        } catch {\n          /* non-JSON response */\n        }\n        onError(te(\"uploadSign\"), detail);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = (await signRes.json()) as {\n        uploadUrl: string;\n        r2Key: string;\n      };\n      const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, {\n        method: \"PUT\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/zip\" },\n        body: file,\n      });\n      if (!putRes.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"uploadFailed\"));\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const attachResult = await attachFile(\n        versionId,\n        r2Key,\n        file.size,\n        \"pending\",\n      );\n      if (!attachResult.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"fallback\"), attachResult.detail ?? null);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      setProgress(\"done\");\n      onBundleUploaded();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(\"[publish] zip upload threw\", err);\n      onError(te(\"fallback\"));\n      setProgress(\"idle\");\n    }\n  }",
  "new_string": "  async function handleFile(file: File) {\n    if (!versionId) {\n      onError(te(\"invalid_input\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    // Extension match is the cheap front-line check; pair it with a MIME\n    // sanity check so a renamed payload (`evil.exe` → `evil.zip`) at\n    // least has to fool the browser too. Some browsers report empty\n    // file.type for drag-drop — only reject when type is set AND wrong.\n    const looksZip =\n      file.name.endsWith(\".zip\") &&\n      (file.type === \"\" ||\n        file.type === \"application/zip\" ||\n        file.type === \"application/x-zip-compressed\");\n    if (!looksZip) {\n      onError(tu(\"errorType\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {\n      onError(tu(\"errorSize\"));\n      return;\n    }\n    setProgress(\"uploading\");\n    setFileName(file.name);\n    try {\n      const signRes = await fetch(\"/api/upload/sign\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          slug: draft.slug,\n          version: draft.version,\n          contentType: \"application/zip\",\n          size: file.size,\n        }),\n      });\n      if (!signRes.ok) {\n        let detail: string | null = null;\n        try {\n          const body = (await signRes.json()) as { detail?: string };\n          detail = body.detail ?? null;\n        } catch {\n          /* non-JSON response */\n        }\n        onError(te(\"uploadSign\"), detail);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      // Validate the response shape at runtime — it's an internal API but\n      // a future change to the response contract should fail loudly here\n      // rather than silently `undefined`-ing into the PUT or attachFile.\n      const signed = (await signRes.json()) as unknown;\n      if (!isSignedUploadResponse(signed)) {\n        onError(te(\"uploadSign\"));\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = signed;\n      // Bound the PUT — 50 MB on a slow link is plausible, but we don't\n      // want a stalled connection to leave the wizard wedged in\n      // \"uploading\" forever. 10 minutes is generous; aborts surface as\n      // the standard upload-failed error message.\n      const controller = new AbortController();\n      const timeoutId = setTimeout(\n        () => controller.abort(),\n        10 * 60 * 1000,\n      );\n      let putRes: Response;\n      try {\n        putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, {\n          method: \"PUT\",\n          headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/zip\" },\n          body: file,\n          signal: controller.signal,\n        });\n      } finally {\n        clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n      }\n      if (!putRes.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"uploadFailed\"));\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      const attachResult = await attachFile(\n        versionId,\n        r2Key,\n        file.size,\n        \"pending\",\n      );\n      if (!attachResult.ok) {\n        onError(te(\"fallback\"), attachResult.detail ?? null);\n        setProgress(\"idle\");\n        return;\n      }\n      setProgress(\"done\");\n      onBundleUploaded();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(\"[publish] zip upload threw\", err);\n      onError(te(\"fallback\"));\n      setProgress(\"idle\");\n    }\n  }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/steps/source.tsx",
  "old_string": "type SourceTab = \"zip\" | \"git\" | \"cli\";",
  "new_string": "type SourceTab = \"zip\" | \"git\" | \"cli\";\n\n// Runtime guard for the `/api/upload/sign` response. The server-side\n// route is internal so we trust it broadly, but a contract change here\n// shouldn't `undefined`-leak into the PUT or attachFile call below.\nfunction isSignedUploadResponse(\n  body: unknown,\n): body is { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string } {\n  if (typeof body !== \"object\" || body === null) return false;\n  const b = body as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return typeof b.uploadUrl === \"string\" && typeof b.r2Key === \"string\";\n}"
}

some browsers leave file.type empty on drag-drop, so we only\n  reject when type is set AND wrong.\n- listing.tsx — Drop the unsafe `as Record<PermissionKey, boolean>`\n  cast in togglePermission; use Partial<Record<…>> directly.\n- listing.tsx — Show \"{n}/8 tags\" hint and disable the tag input once\n  the cap is reached, instead of silently ignoring the 9th add.\n- publish.ts — Lift `versionId` out of the transaction closure so the\n  `row.versionId!` non-null assertion is gone.\n- manifest.ts — Cap `readmeMd` at 16,000 chars; The tests verify the patch-call contract, which is the\n  intent; rerender + DOM assertions doubles surface without finding\n  bugs the contract wouldn't.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

null);\n+          return;\n+        }\n+        setExtensionId(result.extensionId);\n+        setVersionId(result.versionId);\n+      }\n+      setStep(1);\n+    } finally {\n+      setBusy(false);\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  async function saveDraft() {\n+    clearError();\n+    setBusy(true);\n+    try {\n+      if (extensionId) {\n+        const result = await updateDraftExtension(extensionId, draft);\n+        if (!result.ok) {\n+          reportError(describeError(result.error), result.detail ?? > AGENT

Yeah, there are real gaps. The redesign touched code paths that the existing tests don't cover. Three I'd actually write:

1. **`defaultClassification` + create/update write-shape** — `lib/actions/publish.ts` now silently backfills `funcCat`/`subCat` from `category` and persists `sourceMethod` on the version. Neither is tested. If someone re-introduces a notNull constraint or accidentally drops the backfill, the wizard breaks at insert and we'd only catch it in manual QA. Pin both behaviors with a small test in `publish-ownership.test.ts` (or extract `defaultClassification` for a unit test).

2. **`updateDraftExtension` preservation** — there's a load-bearing comment saying "funcCat/subCat are intentionally NOT touched on update — admin curation may have set non-default values." That's a regression-prone invariant: someone tidying the patch object could re-add them and silently revert curated rows. Worth pinning by asserting the captured patch doesn't include either key.

3. **Wizard `saveDraft` no-op gate** — the CodeRabbit-A1 bug (silent redirect drops in-memory edits) just got fixed and has no test. Likely to regress since the gate condition (`extensionId || stepsValid[0]`) is easy to "simplify" away. A small component test that asserts no `router.push` and no action call when both conditions fail would lock it in.

Skip: the source-step abort timeout (hard to test, marginal), the radiogroup a11y (trivial render), and the dead-link removal (covered by the structural change itself).

Want me to write those three?

If someone \"tidies\" the patch object to\n  // include them, this assertion fails before the regression ships.\n  it(\"does not write funcCat or subCat (preserves admin curation)\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n\n    const updatePatches: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];\n    transactionMock.mockImplementation(\n      async (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => {\n        const tx = {\n          update: () => ({\n            set: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n              updatePatches.push(patch);\n              return { where: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) };\n            },\n          }),\n          delete: () => ({ where: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) }),\n          insert: () => ({ values: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) }),\n        };\n        return cb(tx);\n      },\n    );\n\n    const result = await updateDraftExtension(\"ext-1\", validValues);\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: true });\n    for (const patch of updatePatches) {\n      expect(patch).not.toHaveProperty(\"funcCat\");\n      expect(patch).not.toHaveProperty(\"subCat\");\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"createDraftExtension write shape\", () => {\n  // The form schema dropped funcCat/subCat — they're now nullable in the\n  // DB. createDraftExtension backfills them off `category` so filters\n  // keep matching. These tests pin both the mapping and the shape of\n  // the rows it writes (extensions, extensionVersions). If someone\n  // re-introduces a notNull constraint, drops the backfill, or stops\n  // persisting sourceMethod, the wizard breaks at insert and we'd only\n  // catch it in manual QA.\n  const baseValues: ManifestFormValues = {\n    slug: \"my-skill\",\n    name: \"My Skill\",\n    nameZh: \"\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    category: \"skills\",\n    scope: \"personal\",\n    deptId: \"\",\n    tagIds: [],\n    summary: \"Does things.\",\n    taglineZh: \"\",\n    readmeMd: \"\",\n    iconColor: \"indigo\",\n    permissions: {},\n    sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n  };\n\n  // Capture every tx.insert(...).values(...) payload regardless of which\n  // table is being written. We distinguish rows by their distinctive\n  // fields (extensions row carries `slug`, extensionVersions row\n  // carries `version` without `slug`).\n  function captureInserts() {\n    const inserts: Array<Record<string, unknown> | unknown> = [];\n    transactionMock.mockImplementation(\n      async (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => {\n        const tx = {\n          insert: () => ({\n            values: (payload: unknown) => {\n              inserts.push(payload);\n              return Promise.resolve(undefined);\n            },\n          }),\n        };\n        return cb(tx);\n      },\n    );\n    return inserts;\n  }\n\n  function findExtensionsRow(rows: unknown[]) {\n    return rows.find(\n      (r) => typeof r === \"object\" && r !== null && \"slug\" in r,\n    ) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n  }\n  function findVersionRow(rows: unknown[]) {\n    return rows.find(\n      (r) =>\n        typeof r === \"object\" &&\n        r !== null &&\n        \"version\" in r &&\n        !(\"slug\" in r),\n    ) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n  }\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n  });\n\n  it.each([\n    [\"skills\", \"tools\", \"general\"],\n    [\"plugins\", \"tools\", \"general\"],\n    [\"mcp\", \"tools\", \"integrations\"],\n    [\"slash\", \"tools\", \"commands\"],\n  ] as const)(\n    \"backfills funcCat/subCat for category=%s\",\n    async (category, funcCat, subCat) => {\n      const inserts = captureInserts();\n      const result = await createDraftExtension({\n        ...baseValues,\n        category,\n      });\n      expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n      const row = findExtensionsRow(inserts);\n      expect(row).toBeDefined();\n      expect(row?.funcCat).toBe(funcCat);\n      expect(row?.subCat).toBe(subCat);\n    },\n  );\n\n  it(\"persists sourceMethod on the version row\", async () => {\n    const inserts = captureInserts();\n    const result = await createDraftExtension(baseValues);\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    const row = findVersionRow(inserts);\n    expect(row).toBeDefined();\n    expect(row?.sourceMethod).toBe(\"zip\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"writes summary into the tagline column (not a separate summary field)\", async () => {\n    const inserts = captureInserts();\n    await createDraftExtension({ ...baseValues, summary: \"Real-time search\" });\n    const row = findExtensionsRow(inserts);\n    expect(row?.tagline).toBe(\"Real-time search\");\n    // The DB has no `summary` column — make sure we didn't accidentally\n    // start writing one (which would silently no-op against the schema).\n    expect(row).not.toHaveProperty(\"summary\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"persists permissions as the captured object (defaulting to {})\", async () => {\n    const inserts = captureInserts();\n    await createDraftExtension({\n      ...baseValues,\n      permissions: {
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/wizard/index.test.tsx",
  "content": "// @vitest-environment happy-dom\nimport { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { cleanup, render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\n\nvi.mock(\"next-intl\", () => ({\n  useTranslations: () => (key: string) => key,\n  useLocale: () => \"en\",\n}));\n\nconst mockPush = vi.fn();\nvi.mock(\"next/navigation\", () => ({\n  useRouter: () => ({ push: mockPush, replace: vi.fn() }),\n}));\n\n// `Link` is the only thing the wizard imports from the i18n navigation\n// module — stub it as a passthrough <a> so render doesn't trip on the\n// next-intl routing wrapper in tests.\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/i18n/navigation\", () => ({\n  Link: ({\n    children,\n    href,\n    ...rest\n  }: {\n    children: React.ReactNode;\n    href: string;\n  }) => (\n    <a href={href} {...rest}>\n      {children}\n    </a>\n  ),\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/actions/publish\", () => ({\n  createDraftExtension: vi.fn(),\n  updateDraftExtension: vi.fn(),\n  submitForReview: vi.fn(),\n  // attachFile is imported transitively by SourceStep, which the wizard\n  // imports. Stub it so module evaluation doesn't crash even though we\n  // never reach Step 2 in these tests.\n  attachFile: vi.fn(),\n}));\n\nimport { UploadWizard } from \"./index\";\nimport {\n  createDraftExtension,\n  updateDraftExtension,\n} from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\n\nafterEach(cleanup);\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n});\n\ndescribe(\"UploadWizard saveDraft gate\", () => {\n  // CodeRabbit-A1 regression. The original implementation always\n  // redirected to the dashboard on Save Draft, even when there was\n  // nothing to save — the user's in-memory edits silently disappeared.\n  // The fix gates redirect on (extensionId || stepsValid[0]) and\n  // disables the button accordingly. This pins both behaviors.\n  it(\"disables Save Draft and is a no-op when nothing has been persisted\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    render(<UploadWizard />);\n\n    const button = screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"saveDraft\" });\n    expect(button).toBeDisabled();\n\n    await user.click(button);\n\n    expect(mockPush).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    expect(vi.mocked(createDraftExtension)).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    expect(vi.mocked(updateDraftExtension)).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"re-enables Save Draft once a resumed draft is loaded, even if Basics is blank\", () => {\n    // The OR side of the gate: an existing extensionId from resume mode\n    // means there's something on the server to update, regardless of\n    // whether the in-memory Basics fields would pass stepsValid[0].\n    render(\n      <UploadWizard\n        resume={{\n          extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          slug: \"my-skill\",\n          version: \"1.0.0\",\n          name: \"My Skill\",\n          category: \"skills\",\n          scope: \"personal\",\n          bundleUploaded: false,\n          formValues: {\n            slug: \"my-skill\",\n            name: \"My Skill\",\n            nameZh: \"\",\n            // Deliberately blank summary so stepsValid[0] would be false\n            // — only the extensionId guard should be holding the button open.\n            summary: \"\",\n            taglineZh: \"\",\n            version: \"1.0.0\",\n            category: \"skills\",\n            scope: \"personal\",\n            tagIds: [],\n            deptId: \"\",\n            readmeMd: \"\",\n            iconColor: \"indigo\",\n            permissions: {},\n            sourceMethod: \"zip\",\n          },\n        }}\n      />,\n    );\n\n    const button = screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"saveDraft\" });\n    expect(button).not.toBeDisabled();\n  });\n});\n"
}

40:│   ├── publish/
42:│   │   ├── new/page.tsx       # upload wizard (new draft)
43:│   │   └── [id]/edit/page.tsx # upload wizard (resume / edit existing draft)
51:Server components render the static shell, fetch data via Drizzle, and stream HTML. Client components are reserved for interactive bits — filter chips, the install button, the upload wizard, the user menu — and are marked with `"use client"`. The pattern is "server frame, client islands."
96:- **Versions** — flat changelog from `listExtensionVersions` (status `ready` only, ordered by `publishedAt DESC`), with the latest entry tagged "Current".
99:The sidebar has two cards. `<ExtAboutCard>` renders publisher / version / size / license / updated / scope plus the homepage and repo links — every URL passes through `safeExternalUrl()` (allowlists `http(s):`, drops anything else) before reaching `href`. `<ExtRelatedList>` shows the four related extensions; it returns `null` when empty so an empty card never shows.
103:### Publish
125:   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
133:R2 bundle keys are `bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip` (`bundleKey()` in `lib/storage/r2.ts`). Two consequences worth knowing: (1) the wizard locks `slug` and `version` in resume/edit mode whenever a bundle has been uploaded — letting either change would orphan the bundle at the old key — and (2) discarding a draft only deletes DB rows; the R2 object is left for bucket lifecycle to garbage-collect.
137:The dashboard at `/[locale]/publish` shows each draft's stage (`Needs bundle upload` / `Ready to submit` / `Awaiting scan` / etc., derived from `extensionVersions.status` + `bundleFileId` via `rowAction()` in `lib/publish/row-action.ts`). Rows whose latest version is `pending` are clickable and link into `/publish/[id]/edit`, which loads the draft via `getDraft()` and mounts the same `<UploadWizard>` in resume mode — Step 1 form pre-filled, slug/version locked, initial step derived from whether a bundle is uploaded. Step 1's submit calls `updateDraftExtension()` instead of `createDraftExtension()`; the action refuses (`version_not_editable`) once status leaves `pending`. Rejected rows aren't clickable but surface the scan reason inline (extracted from `files.scanReport` via `extractScanReason()` in `lib/publish/scan-report.ts`) so the dashboard isn't a dead end for failed scans.
147:- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
164:Both surfaces are thin wrappers around `recordInstall` in `lib/installs/record.ts`, which owns: extension lookup (by `id` from the web button or `slug` from the CLI), version resolution (an omitted version means "latest published" — `extension_versions` ordered by `publishedAt DESC` among `status='ready'`), the `installs` row insert, the `installed` collection upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` bump — all three writes go through `db.transaction`. Each call records a row, so `downloadsCount` is total install events; the `installed` collection is a separate, idempotent membership concept used by the UI's "Installed" view. Failures throw a typed `InstallError` (`extension_not_found`, `no_published_version`); wrappers translate to their surface (action union or HTTP status).
197:| `/api/upload/sign` | Web-internal — issues an R2 presigned PUT URL for the upload wizard |
205:publish wizard ──→ extensions (draft) ──→ files + extensionVersions ──→ Inngest scan
207:                          extensions (published, search_vector) ←──── reindex-search
216:Every write that affects what users see in the listing (publish, install) ends with a `revalidateTag` so the next browse render hits fresh data.
```

100	
101	The share URL on the hero is composed from `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` rather than the request `Host` header, since spoofable forwarded headers would let an attacker control what gets copied to clipboard.
102	
103	### Publish
104	
105	```text
106	Browser                          Vercel                          R2          Inngest
107	   │ form submit                    │                              │             │
108	   ├──── createDraftExtension ────► server action  ──► extensions row (status=draft)
109	   │                                │                              │             │
110	   ├──── upload bundle ─────────────│─── presigned PUT URL ───────►│             │
111	   │                                │                              │             │
112	   ├──── attachFile(versionId) ────► server action  ──► files row + extensionVersion
113	   │                                │                              │             │
114	   ├──── submitForReview ──────────► server action  ──► sendEvent("extension/scan.requested")
115	   │                                │                              │             │
116	   │                                │                              │  scan-bundle:
117	   │                                │                              │   download from R2,
118	   │                                │                              │   sha-256 checksum,
119	   │                                │                              │   parse manifest.toml,
120	   │                                │                              │   validate schema,
121	   │                                │                              │   mark version ready/rejected,
122	   │                                │                              │   sendEvent("extension/index.requested")
123	   │                                │                              │             │
124	   │                                │                              │  reindex-search:
125	   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
126	   │                                │                              │   revalidateTag("extensions")
127	```
128	
129	The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.
130	
131	A note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.
132	
133	R2 bundle keys are `bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip` (`bundleKey()` in `lib/storage/r2.ts`). Two consequences worth knowing: (1) the wizard locks `slug` and `version` in resume/edit mode whenever a bundle has been uploaded — letting either change would orphan the bundle at the old key — and (2) discarding a draft only deletes DB rows; the R2 object is left for bucket lifecycle to garbage-collect.
134	
135	### Resume, edit, and discard
136	
137	The dashboard at `/[locale]/publish` shows each draft's stage (`Needs bundle upload` / `Ready to submit` / `Awaiting scan` / etc., derived from `extensionVersions.status` + `bundleFileId` via `rowAction()` in `lib/publish/row-action.ts`). Rows whose latest version is `pending` are clickable and link into `/publish/[id]/edit`, which loads the draft via `getDraft()` and mounts the same `<UploadWizard>` in resume mode — Step 1 form pre-filled, slug/version locked, initial step derived from whether a bundle is uploaded. Step 1's submit calls `updateDraftExtension()` instead of `createDraftExtension()`; the action refuses (`version_not_editable`) once status leaves `pending`. Rejected rows aren't clickable but surface the scan reason inline (extracted from `files.scanReport` via `extractScanReason()` in `lib/publish/scan-report.ts`) so the dashboard isn't a dead end for failed scans.
138	
139	Each draft row also gets a `<DiscardButton>` that calls `discardDraft()` — owner-checked, draft-visibility-only, hard delete (FK cascades clean up versions/tags/files-row links). `getDraft` and `discardDraft` deliberately collapse non-owner into `not_found` so the public action contract can't be used to probe existence.
140	
141	### Version state transitions
142	
143	`lib/extensions/state.ts` owns every transition of `extensionVersions.status`, `files.scanStatus`, and `extensions.visibility`. Three functions:
144	
145	- `submit(versionId)` — moves a version into `scanning`. Idempotent: accepts `pending` *or* `scanning` as the source state, so a Step 3 retry after a failed `inngest.send` can re-queue the scan instead of getting stuck. Versions in `ready` / `rejected` reject the transition.
146	- `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` — applies the outcome of a bundle scan in one transaction; success sets `files.scanStatus='clean' + version.status='ready' + checksumSha256`, failure sets `'flagged' + 'rejected'`. The job calls this once with a discriminated `result`.
147	- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
148	
149	`extensions.search_vector` is a Postgres `GENERATED ALWAYS ... STORED` column (see `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql`); no application code writes it. The `reindex-search` job's earlier hand-rolled `to_tsvector` UPDATE was dead — the generated column always reflects the source content.
150	
151	### Install (CLI path)
152	
153	```text
154	agentcenter install my-skill
```

# AgentCenter Extension Manifest Spec

Every extension bundle (`.zip`) **must** contain a `manifest.toml` at the archive root.
The manifest declares identity, discoverability, compatibility, and per-agent install targets.

---

## Top-level sections

### `[extension]` — required

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `slug` | string | ✓ | URL-safe identifier, unique across the registry. Pattern: `[a-z0-9-]+`, max 64 chars. |
| `name` | string | ✓ | Display name in English. |
| `nameZh` | string | | Display name in Chinese (optional bilingual). |
| `version` | string | ✓ | Semver (e.g. `1.0.0`). |
| `category` | enum | ✓ | One of `skills`, `mcp`, `slash`, `plugins`. |
| `scope` | enum | ✓ | Audience: `personal`, `org`, `enterprise`. |
| `license` | string | | SPDX identifier (e.g. `MIT`, `Apache-2.0`). Omit for proprietary. |
| `description` | string | ✓ | Short description in English, max 280 chars. |
| `descriptionZh` | string | | Short description in Chinese. |
| `tagline` | string | | One-line tagline shown in the featured banner. |
| `tags` | string[] | | Tag slugs from the registry vocabulary (see Tags below). |

### `[categorization]` — required

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `funcCat` | enum | Functional category: `workTask`, `business`, `tools`. |
| `subCat` | string | L1 subcategory key (e.g. `softDev`, `systemDesign`). |
| `l2` | string | L2 sub-subcategory key (e.g. `backend`, `frontend`). Optional. |

### `[links]` — optional

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `homepage` | URL | Public homepage or docs site. |
| `repo` | URL | Source repository (GitHub, GitLab, etc.). |

### `[compatibility]` — optional

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `minAgentVersion` | string | Minimum agent version required (semver, e.g. `0.8.0`). |
| `os` | string[] | Restrict to OS list: `darwin`, `linux`, `windows`. Omit = all. |

### `[install.<agent>]` — at least one required

Declares per-agent install destinations. The CLI reads the profile that matches
`agentcenter config get agent` (default: `claude`).

**Skills** (`category = "skills"`):

```toml
[install.claude]
skills = "~/.claude/skills/{slug}"
```

**MCP Servers** (`category = "mcp"`):

```toml
[install.claude]
mcpConfig = "~/.claude/claude_desktop_config.json"
mcpKey    = "{slug}"
```

**Slash Commands** (`category = "slash"`):

```toml
[install.claude]
commands = "~/.claude/commands/{slug}.md"
```

**Plugins** (`category = "plugins"`):

```toml
[install.claude]
plugins = "~/.claude/plugins/{slug}"
```

Path tokens:

| Token | Expands to |
|---|---|
| `{slug}` | Extension slug |
| `{version}` | Installed version |
| `{agent}` | Active agent profile name |

Optional `[install.<agent>.postInstall]`:

```toml
[install.claude.postInstall]
message = "Set your API key: agentcenter config set {slug}.apiKey <key>"
```

---

## Full example

```toml
[extension]
slug        = "web-search-pro"
name        = "Web Search Pro"
nameZh      = "网页搜索增强"
version     = "1.2.0"
category    = "skills"
scope       = "org"
license     = "MIT"
description = "Real-time web search integration for Claude via the Brave Search API."
descriptionZh = "为 Claude 接入 Brave 搜索 API，提供实时网页搜索能力。"
tagline     = "Search the web, right inside Claude."
tags        = ["search", "real-time", "stable"]

[categorization]
funcCat = "workTask"
subCat  = "softDev"
l2      = "backend"

[links]
homepage = "https://example.com/web-search-pro"
repo     = "https://github.com/example/web-search-pro"

[compatibility]
minAgentVersion = "0.8.0"
os = ["darwin", "linux"]

[install.claude]
skills = "~/.claude/skills/{slug}"

[install.claude.postInstall]
message = "Add your Brave API key: agentcenter config set web-search-pro.apiKey <your-key>"
```

---

## Bundle structure

```
web-search-pro-1.2.0.zip
├── manifest.toml       ← required, at archive root
├── skill.md            ← skill definition (for category=skills)
├── README.md           ← optional, rendered on detail page
└── assets/             ← optional supporting files
```

For `category = mcp`, include a `server.js` / `server.py` or equivalent binary.
For `category = slash`, include the command markdown file(s).
For `category = plugins`, include the plugin entry point per agent conventions.

---

## Tags vocabulary

Tags are defined in `lib/tags.ts`. Current set (32 tags):

`ai-native` `analytics` `automation` `ci-cd` `cloud` `code-gen` `code-review`
`data` `deployment` `devops` `document` `embedded` `enterprise` `grpc`
`http` `infra` `integration` `llm` `monitoring` `mqtt` `network` `open-source`
`performance` `personal` `real-time` `search` `security` `stable` `testing`
`translation` `ui` `version-control`

---

## Versioning rules

- Versions must be valid semver.
- Re-publishing the same `slug@version` is rejected (immutable releases).
- A new `version` is required for every update.
- The registry always serves the **latest published** version at `GET /api/v1/extensions/:slug`.
  Future `/api/v1/extensions/:slug/versions` endpoint will list history.
```

Yes — one file has real drift. `docs/architecture.md` has three load-bearing spots that no longer match the code:

1. **Publish flow diagram** (lines 105-127) still shows the 3-step pipeline. Needs a 4-step Basics → Source → Listing → Review pass and the auto-publish branch on personal scope.
2. **Resume/Edit paragraph** (line 137) says slug+version lock "whenever a bundle has been uploaded". That gate is now `extensionId` existence — CodeRabbit-A2 fix on PR #24.
3. **Version State Transitions** (line 146) describes `recordScanResult` as `clean → ready` for everyone. It now branches on `extensions.scope` (personal auto-publishes inline; org/enterprise still stays at `ready`). This is the most consequential omission — the auto-publish path is invisible from the docs.

Also worth a one-line mention of the new schema fields (`permissions`, `sourceMethod`, `sourceMeta`) and the `defaultClassification` backfill, since those are the invariants future code touches.

The other docs are fine: `manifest-spec.md` describes the bundle TOML, which still requires `description`/`funcCat`/`subCat` — the wizard's slimmer form is web-side only and doesn't change what bundles ship. `api.md` response shape is unchanged. `cli.md` is downstream-only. `plan.md` was already updated in PR #24.

111	   │                                │                              │             │
112	   ├──── attachFile(versionId) ────► server action  ──► files row + extensionVersion
113	   │                                │                              │             │
114	   ├──── submitForReview ──────────► server action  ──► sendEvent("extension/scan.requested")
115	   │                                │                              │             │
116	   │                                │                              │  scan-bundle:
117	   │                                │                              │   download from R2,
118	   │                                │                              │   sha-256 checksum,
119	   │                                │                              │   parse manifest.toml,
120	   │                                │                              │   validate schema,
121	   │                                │                              │   mark version ready/rejected,
122	   │                                │                              │   sendEvent("extension/index.requested")
123	   │                                │                              │             │
124	   │                                │                              │  reindex-search:
125	   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
126	   │                                │                              │   revalidateTag("extensions")
127	```
128	
129	The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.
130	
131	A note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.
132	
133	R2 bundle keys are `bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip` (`bundleKey()` in `lib/storage/r2.ts`). Two consequences worth knowing: (1) the wizard locks `slug` and `version` in resume/edit mode whenever a bundle has been uploaded — letting either change would orphan the bundle at the old key — and (2) discarding a draft only deletes DB rows; the R2 object is left for bucket lifecycle to garbage-collect.
134	
135	### Resume, edit, and discard
136	
137	The dashboard at `/[locale]/publish` shows each draft's stage (`Needs bundle upload` / `Ready to submit` / `Awaiting scan` / etc., derived from `extensionVersions.status` + `bundleFileId` via `rowAction()` in `lib/publish/row-action.ts`). Rows whose latest version is `pending` are clickable and link into `/publish/[id]/edit`, which loads the draft via `getDraft()` and mounts the same `<UploadWizard>` in resume mode — Step 1 form pre-filled, slug/version locked, initial step derived from whether a bundle is uploaded. Step 1's submit calls `updateDraftExtension()` instead of `createDraftExtension()`; the action refuses (`version_not_editable`) once status leaves `pending`. Rejected rows aren't clickable but surface the scan reason inline (extracted from `files.scanReport` via `extractScanReason()` in `lib/publish/scan-report.ts`) so the dashboard isn't a dead end for failed scans.
138	
139	Each draft row also gets a `<DiscardButton>` that calls `discardDraft()` — owner-checked, draft-visibility-only, hard delete (FK cascades clean up versions/tags/files-row links). `getDraft` and `discardDraft` deliberately collapse non-owner into `not_found` so the public action contract can't be used to probe existence.
140	
141	### Version state transitions
142	
143	`lib/extensions/state.ts` owns every transition of `extensionVersions.status`, `files.scanStatus`, and `extensions.visibility`. Three functions:
144	
145	- `submit(versionId)` — moves a version into `scanning`. Idempotent: accepts `pending` *or* `scanning` as the source state, so a Step 3 retry after a failed `inngest.send` can re-queue the scan instead of getting stuck. Versions in `ready` / `rejected` reject the transition.
146	- `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` — applies the outcome of a bundle scan in one transaction; success sets `files.scanStatus='clean' + version.status='ready' + checksumSha256`, failure sets `'flagged' + 'rejected'`. The job calls this once with a discriminated `result`.
147	- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
148	
149	`extensions.search_vector` is a Postgres `GENERATED ALWAYS ... STORED` column (see `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql`); no application code writes it. The `reindex-search` job's earlier hand-rolled `to_tsvector` UPDATE was dead — the generated column always reflects the source content.
150	
151	### Install (CLI path)
152	
153	```text
154	agentcenter install my-skill
```

}
